17.2 The checks that matter most

Anchor orthogonality (4). The maximum off-diagonal |r| among the kept anchors is 0.8199, just under the 0.82 suppression threshold. This proves the pruning actually ran rather than being declared.

Sigma causality (6). The correlation between the trade-time sigma estimate and the forward move is +0.0098. If the sigma estimator leaked the forward window this number would be large; it is not, so the volatility gate is causal.

One trade per cell (7). Zero duplicate outbound cells. Without this, 208 overlapping anchors would produce tens of thousands of correlated "trades" and every statistic would be inflated by a factor that depends on the overlap structure.

Friction monotonicity (8). The four tiers decrease strictly (-86.7 → -209.0 → -331.4 → -494.5). A non-monotone ladder would mean the cost model has a sign error.

Live parity, honestly reported (12). Recall 0.240, precision 0.295, IoU 0.152. The gatekeeper passes this check because the artifact states the ceiling instead of hiding it - a distinction worth making explicit in a report that elsewhere accuses the pipeline of self-flattery.

No credentials (17). The artifact scan finds no API token, and the check was written so that its own source cannot trigger a false positive (the needle is split across two literals).

17.3 What the gatekeeper is not

It is a consistency and integrity verifier. It does not re-run the backtest, it does not independently re-derive the vault, and it cannot tell you whether the payouts are real. Those three gaps are filled - to the extent they can be - by the definition diagnostic (Chapter 15), the two independent node audits (Chapter 7), and the live tester once connectivity returns (Chapter 16).

18. Reproducibility

Every number in this report is produced by a script in the repository, from artifacts whose checksums are published in Appendix E. This chapter gives the exact procedure, including the places where the environment fights back.

18.1 Hardware and roles

NodeAddressOSRole
X-Flip 14 (master)192.168.150.199Windows 11 Orchestration, Phase 1 merge, Phase 2 wiring, Phase 3 shard 0 and merge, documentation build.
HP 840 G7192.168.150.126Windows 11 Phase 1 shard, Phase 3 shard 1, independent node audit (Python 3.14).
Kali Dell E5270192.168.150.166Kali Linux Phase 1 shard, Phase 3 shard 2 (CSV output - no duckdb), independent node audit.

The master's LAN address is assigned by DHCP and moved three times during the project (.237 → .157 → .199). All worker-side scripts take the master address as an argument rather than hard-coding it.

18.2 Transport

A single-threaded TCP command server on port 8080 accepts newline-delimited jobs and returns stdout/stderr with a 30-second hard timeout. Files are served over the same port: workers request /artifacts/phase2/... and receive the artefact directly, which is why the 667 MB Phase 3 input set can be distributed without a shared filesystem. Directory junctions on the master (out\phase2, out\phase3) present local paths to remote workers without copying.

Quoting is the fragile part. Multi-line shell commands containing nested quotes are routinely mangled by the transport, so every non-trivial job is shipped as a .py file and invoked by path. This convention is worth preserving in any successor system.

18.3 The parquet problem

Windows Application Control blocks PyArrow's native extensions and also blocks fastparquet's cencoding.pyd. All parquet I/O therefore goes through pqcompat.py, which tries duckdb, then pyarrow, then fastparquet, and exports both read_parquet/write_parquet and a duckdb-backed write_parquet fallback. Kali has no duckdb, so its shard writes CSV; the merger globs both extensions. This is why every Phase 3 script imports pqcompat and never pandas.read_parquet directly.

18.4 Determinism

The Phase 3 shard split is a pure function of the pruned anchor list, so any machine can recompute which anchors belong to which shard. All three shards independently computed the sigma quantile edges and printed the identical six values (0.1526, 0.1755, 0.1772, 0.1787, 0.1805, 0.1912 bps). Torch training for the live bridge uses a fixed seed and reports the validation cosine; the shipped weights are committed so the tester does not need to retrain.

18.5 Rebuilding the report

python C:\cluster\tools\build_docs.py

The builder reads the parquet/CSV/JSON/XLSX artifacts, renders docs/PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION.html, and prints the section list. Rendering to PDF is a Chrome headless print:

chrome --headless --disable-gpu --no-pdf-header-footer ^
       --print-to-pdf=docs\PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION.pdf docs\PROJECT_DOCUMENTATION.html

18.6 Repository hygiene

The published repository is private and excludes: the 3.4 GB Fourier archive and every other .npz/.npy/.pt artefact (checksummed in docs/DATA_MANIFEST.json instead), the MT5 backtest clone, directory junctions, the live SQLite journal, and both credential files. The Phase 3 brief ships as a redacted copy with zero token matches, verified by the gatekeeper.

19. Limitations

A negative result is only as trustworthy as its weakest assumption. These are the weak ones, each with the direction in which it could bite.

19.1 The payout assumption is unverified, and internally inconsistent

Four payouts (1.953, 2.150, 2.427, 1.550) drive every dollar figure. Deriv returned HTTP 520 on the WebSocket endpoint from all three nodes, so none of them could be checked against live pricing. The ledger then exposed a contradiction that proves the table is wrong in at least one cell: applying the ONETOUCH payout to the measured ONETOUCH win rate gives 0.7166 × 2.15 − 1 = +54% per trade over 3,941 trades, while the payout-independent sigma accounting of the same trades yields −0.02σ per trade. A real 2.15× contract cannot be won 71.7% of the time at a 0.75σ barrier; the payout and the barrier were specified independently. Chapter 14.4 reports both number systems side by side for this reason. Every conclusion that does not depend on the payouts is stated in sigma units; the dollar figures should be read as illustrative only.

19.2 The definition artefact is not a data bug

The max(protected, directional) statistic can be recomputed exactly from the vault; the recomputation reproduces the stored maximum almost perfectly. What fails is the inference from "this column is high" to "this anchor is tradeable". Nothing in the pipeline was corrupted; the aggregation rule was wrong. That distinction matters because it means a rebuild cannot be trusted unless the aggregation rule itself is changed.

19.3 The null test inherits the bias

Because the permutation null re-applies the same max() selection, the p-value of 0.0025 tests consistency, not edge. A future test should permute the outcome and evaluate a pre-registered single statistic, or apply a max-statistic correction with the null distribution of the max.

19.4 Similarity matching is barely selective

37% of arbitrary outbound cells match at least one of the 893 anchors within the accepted band. Any strategy that treats "triggered" as informative must be compared with that base rate. Phase 3 does not repair this; it merely stops double-counting the same cell.

19.5 Live parity is 0.24

The whitening parameters from Phase 2 were never persisted, so the live matcher fires on about a quarter of backtest triggers. The live system is therefore a subset sampler of a strategy that is already unprofitable. Even if the subset were the best quarter, the backtest provides no evidence that it is.

19.6 The hour filter is hindsight

Fourteen blocked hours were selected on the same period they filter. Removing them flatters the portfolio. It is reported, not hidden, but it is not a defensible live rule as constructed.

19.7 One instrument, one tick source, one era

Everything is Jump 10 midpoints over 1,028.5 days. There is no cross-instrument validation and no post-2023 out-of-sample period. Jump 10's volatility parameter is stationary by design, which makes it the easiest possible case; a negative result here is strong evidence, but a positive one would have generalised poorly.

19.8 Random-split evaluation in CZET v2

CZET v2 used random row splits, which allow near-duplicate leakage. Its Spearman 0.511 is therefore an optimistic bound on ranking skill. It does not affect the Phase 3 conclusion, which uses a strictly forward replay.

19.9 Barrier touches are modelled at tick resolution

A barrier is deemed hit when the tick midpoint crosses it. In reality a touch option settles on the quoted price stream including spread, so a modelled touch at +0.75σ may not have been a real touch. This biases ONETOUCH upward, which is the direction that flatters the result.

19.10 Deduplication changes the strategy

Keeping one trade per outbound cell is necessary for valid statistics, but it is not what a live system would do: a live system would trade every trigger that passes the guards, and the deduplicated portfolio is therefore an idealised, non-overlapping sample of that behaviour.

19.11 Fixed-stake survival is dominated by the stop

The $400 session stop and the ruin clamp both fired in the fixed-$5 simulation, so the terminal balance of 10,901.5 measures the stop, not the edge. It should not be quoted as a performance figure without that caveat.

19.12 The 132-dimensional space is nearly collinear

Random anchor pairs have a mean cosine near 0.86. Any threshold in that band is discriminating on a narrow slice of a compressed distribution, which makes match counts sensitive to small changes in the whitening. This is the mechanism behind 19.4 and 19.5, and it is the single structural weakness the project did not overcome.

20. Conclusions and roadmap

What was learned, what was built, and what would have to change for the question to be worth reopening.

20.1 Conclusions

  1. The infrastructure works and is reusable. A three-node cluster processed 88.7 million ticks into 591,171 cells, built a Fourier encoding with exact reconstruction, produced a 241,703-edge relational graph, trained a transformer, ran a 398-anchor distributed backtest, and verified 28 independent claims - all with reproducible artefacts and published checksums.
  2. The Phase 2 edge was an artefact of its own definition. A from-scratch recomputation of the same quantity on the same anchors yields 0.4884 where the vault reports a mean of 0.5104 and a maximum of 1.000. The correlation between the stored column and the recomputation is -0.049.
  3. Real conditional structure exists but is small. CZET v2 ranks continuation better than raw similarity (Spearman 0.511 vs 0.101, precision 4.34% vs a 1.73% base rate) while explaining almost no variance (R² 0.028). Ranking skill is not the same thing as a tradeable edge.
  4. Nothing survives conversion to a trade. Net win rate 0.4519 against a 51.20% breakeven, profit factor 0.9580, cumulative equity -209.0 sigma net, Monte Carlo P(positive) 0.1864.
  5. The live system is complete but unexercised. Seven guards, a SQLite calibration journal, an honest 0.24 parity ceiling, and a provider-side WebSocket outage that no client-side change could fix.

20.2 Recommendations

  • Do not deploy. The recommendation is unambiguous and is encoded in the shipped config as portfolio_verdict = NEGATIVE_NET_OF_FRICTION.
  • Do not re-run the same pipeline hoping for a different number. The result is a property of the definition, not of the run.
  • Keep the infrastructure. The cluster, the Fourier encoding, the graph builder and the gatekeeper pattern are instrument-independent and reusable for any tick series.
  • Fix the statistics before adding models. A pre-registered single outcome, a multiplicity-corrected selection, and a held-out period are prerequisites for any future vault.

20.3 Roadmap, in priority order

#Work itemWhy it would change the answer
1Persist whitening parameters and rebuild the live matcher to parity Raises live recall from 0.24 to ~1.0, making the tester a true out-of-sample test rather than a subset sampler.
2Verify live payouts via a working Deriv endpoint (alternate edge, proxy, or regional endpoint)Converts every sigma-unit conclusion into a dollar conclusion and resolves the one assumption that could flip the sign.
3Pre-register a single outcome statistic and rebuild the vault with no max()Removes the selection artefact at the source; the resulting vault will be much smaller and, for the first time, interpretable.
4Add a genuinely held-out period (2024+) and a second instrument Tests whether any residual conditional structure generalises; the current corpus cannot answer that.
5Replace cosine similarity with a discriminative metric trained on forward outcomesAttacks the collinearity directly: the current metric compresses 591k cells into a narrow band, which is why matching is barely selective.
6Model barrier settlement on the quote stream, not the midpoint Removes a known upward bias on ONETOUCH, the only contract with a positive realised EV.
7Replace the hindsight hour filter with an expanding-window estimate Makes the live rule causal; currently it is a diagnostic masquerading as a filter.
8Re-derive the payout table from the barrier geometry (or obtain live quotes) Removes the +54%-per-trade inconsistency between the contract accounting and the sigma accounting, and makes every dollar figure in this report meaningful.

20.4 Closing

The programme asked a narrow question and answered it. The value is not in the answer - it is negative - but in the audit trail: a specific, reproducible mechanism (max() over two noisy win-rate estimates) that manufactures apparent edges, together with the instruments needed to detect it. Any future vault built on the same pattern will fail the same way, and this report gives the diagnostic that shows it.

Appendix A - source listings

The complete source of every script that produced a number in this report. The listings are reproduced verbatim from the repository at build time, so they cannot drift from the artefacts.

A.1 Phase 1 - corpus construction

phase1/scripts/phase1_worker.py - per-node 150-tick cell builder and Fourier encoder (477 lines, 17.9 KB)
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import math
import gc
import shutil
import subprocess
import traceback
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

CELL_SIZE = 150


def log(msg):
    print(f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}", flush=True)


def json_dump(path, obj):
    tmp = str(path) + ".tmp"
    with open(tmp, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(obj, f, indent=2)
    os.replace(tmp, path)


def upload_file(local_path, url, retries=5):
    curl = shutil.which("curl") or shutil.which("curl.exe")
    if not curl:
        raise RuntimeError("curl not found")
    last = None
    for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
        p = subprocess.run(
            [curl, "-sS", "--fail", "--retry", "3", "--retry-delay", "2",
             "-X", "PUT", "--data-binary", "@" + str(local_path), url],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3600,
        )
        if p.returncode == 0:
            return p.stdout.strip()
        last = (p.returncode, p.stdout.strip(), p.stderr.strip())
        log(f"upload attempt {attempt} failed for {os.path.basename(str(local_path))}: {last}")
        time.sleep(3)
    raise RuntimeError(f"upload failed: {last}")


def download_file(url, dest, expected_size=None, retries=4):
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(dest), exist_ok=True)
    part = dest + ".part"
    last = None
    for attempt in range(1, retries + 1):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "phase1-worker"})
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as r, open(part, "wb") as f:
                total = int(r.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
                got = 0
                t0 = time.time()
                last_log = t0
                while True:
                    chunk = r.read(1024 * 1024)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    f.write(chunk)
                    got += len(chunk)
                    now = time.time()
                    if now - last_log >= 5:
                        mb = got / (1024 * 1024)
                        speed = mb / max(now - t0, 1e-9)
                        log(f"    downloaded {mb:.1f} MB / {total/(1024*1024):.1f} MB ({speed:.1f} MB/s)")
                        last_log = now
            if expected_size is not None and got != expected_size:
                raise IOError(f"size mismatch got {got} expected {expected_size}")
            os.replace(part, dest)
            return got
        except Exception as e:
            last = e
            try:
                if os.path.exists(part):
                    os.remove(part)
            except OSError:
                pass
            log(f"download attempt {attempt} failed for {os.path.basename(dest)}: {e}")
            time.sleep(2 * attempt)
    raise RuntimeError(f"download failed: {url}: {last}")


def upload_json(obj, filename, upload_url):
    tmp = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), filename)
    json_dump(tmp, obj)
    try:
        upload_file(tmp, upload_url + filename)
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"progress upload failed: {e}")


def compute_epicyclic_fourier_transform(cell_prices, K=75):
    n_cells, M = cell_prices.shape
    if M != CELL_SIZE:
        raise ValueError(f"Expected {CELL_SIZE} ticks per cell, got {M}")

    p_open = cell_prices[:, 0:1]
    p_close = cell_prices[:, -1:]
    p_high = cell_prices.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    p_low = cell_prices.min(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    p_mean = cell_prices.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    p_std_raw = cell_prices.std(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    p_std = np.where(p_std_raw < 1e-12, 1.0, p_std_raw)

    y_norm = (cell_prices - p_mean) / p_std

    t_linear = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    bridge_slope = y_norm[:, -1:] - y_norm[:, 0:1]
    y_bridge = y_norm - (y_norm[:, 0:1] + t_linear * bridge_slope)

    theta = 2.0 * np.pi * np.arange(M, dtype=np.float64) / M
    z_loop = np.cos(theta)[None, :] + 1j * y_bridge

    fft_full = np.fft.fft(z_loop, axis=1) / M
    freqs = np.fft.fftfreq(M, d=1.0 / M).astype(int)

    if K >= 75:
        k_indices = freqs.copy()
        c_k = fft_full
        fft_recon_buf = fft_full
    else:
        k_indices = np.array([k for k in range(-K, K + 1)], dtype=int)
        col_idx = [int(np.where(freqs == k)[0][0]) for k in k_indices]
        c_k = fft_full[:, col_idx]
        fft_recon_buf = np.zeros_like(fft_full)
        for idx, k in enumerate(k_indices):
            f_col = int(np.where(freqs == k)[0][0])
            fft_recon_buf[:, f_col] = c_k[:, idx]

    radii = np.abs(c_k)
    phases = np.angle(c_k)
    omegas = 2.0 * np.pi * k_indices / M

    z_reconstructed = np.fft.ifft(fft_recon_buf * M, axis=1)
    y_bridge_rec = z_reconstructed.imag
    y_norm_rec = y_bridge_rec + (y_norm[:, 0:1] + t_linear * bridge_slope)
    prices_reconstructed = (y_norm_rec * p_std) + p_mean

    abs_err = np.abs(cell_prices - prices_reconstructed)
    max_abs_err = np.max(abs_err, axis=1)
    rmse_err = np.sqrt(np.mean((cell_prices - prices_reconstructed) ** 2, axis=1))
    norm_err = np.sqrt(np.mean((y_norm - y_norm_rec) ** 2, axis=1))

    return {
        "p_open": p_open.ravel(),
        "p_close": p_close.ravel(),
        "p_high": p_high.ravel(),
        "p_low": p_low.ravel(),
        "p_mean": p_mean.ravel(),
        "p_std": p_std.ravel(),
        "y_open_norm": y_norm[:, 0].ravel(),
        "bridge_slope": bridge_slope.ravel(),
        "c_k": c_k,
        "radii": radii,
        "phases": phases,
        "omegas": omegas,
        "k_indices": k_indices,
        "rmse_residual": rmse_err,
        "max_tick_error": max_abs_err,
        "norm_rmse": norm_err,
    }


def main():
    if len(sys.argv) != 3 or sys.argv[1] != "--job":
        print("usage: phase1_worker.py --job job.json", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2

    job_path = sys.argv[2]
    with open(job_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        job = json.load(f)

    node = job["node"]
    data_dir = job["data_dir"]
    files = job["files"]
    file_rows = job["file_rows"]
    carry_file = job.get("carry_file")
    carry_rows = int(job.get("carry_rows", 0))
    carry_file_rows = int(job.get("carry_file_rows", 0))
    K = int(job.get("harmonics_K", 75))
    out_dir = job["output_dir"]
    upload_url = job.get("upload_url")
    do_upload = bool(job.get("upload", False))
    global_tick_cursor = int(job["global_start_tick"])
    global_cell_id = int(job["global_start_cell"])

    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs(data_dir, exist_ok=True)

    download_files = job.get("download_files", [])
    data_url_base = job.get("data_url_base")
    if download_files:
        if not data_url_base:
            raise ValueError("download_files present but data_url_base missing")
        for item in download_files:
            name = item["name"]
            size = int(item["size"])
            dest = os.path.join(data_dir, name)
            if os.path.isfile(dest) and os.path.getsize(dest) == size:
                log(f"data already present: {name} ({size:,} bytes)")
                continue
            log(f"downloading {name} ({size:,} bytes) from {data_url_base}")
            download_file(data_url_base + name, dest, expected_size=size)

    run_started = time.time()
    progress = {
        "node": node,
        "status": "starting",
        "cells_done": 0,
        "ticks_done": 0,
        "files_done": 0,
        "files_total": len(files),
        "global_start_tick": global_tick_cursor,
        "global_start_cell": global_cell_id,
        "started_utc": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
    }
    if do_upload and upload_url:
        upload_json(progress, f"progress_{node}.json", upload_url)

    def progress_update(**kw):
        progress.update(kw)
        if do_upload and upload_url:
            upload_json(progress, f"progress_{node}.json", upload_url)

    carry_prices = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    carry_times = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

    if carry_rows > 0:
        if not carry_file:
            raise ValueError("carry_rows > 0 but carry_file is null")
        cfp = os.path.join(data_dir, carry_file)
        log(f"reading carryover {carry_rows} ticks from {carry_file}")
        cdf = pd.read_csv(cfp, usecols=["epoch_ms", "bid", "ask"],
                          dtype={"epoch_ms": "int64", "bid": "float64", "ask": "float64"})
        if carry_file_rows and len(cdf) != carry_file_rows:
            raise ValueError(f"carry file row count {len(cdf)} != expected {carry_file_rows}")
        if len(cdf) < carry_rows:
            raise ValueError("carry file shorter than carry_rows")
        ctail = cdf.tail(carry_rows)
        carry_prices = ((ctail["bid"].to_numpy(np.float64) + ctail["ask"].to_numpy(np.float64)) / 2.0)
        carry_times = ctail["epoch_ms"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        del cdf, ctail
        log(f"carryover ready: {len(carry_prices)} ticks, first={carry_times[0]}, last={carry_times[-1]}")

    for old in os.listdir(out_dir):
        if old.startswith(f"chunk_{node}_") or old.startswith(f"summary_{node}"):
            try:
                os.remove(os.path.join(out_dir, old))
            except OSError:
                pass

    chunks = []
    k_indices_ref = None
    total_cells = 0
    total_ticks = 0
    max_tick_error_all = 0.0
    max_norm_rmse_all = 0.0
    cells_under_1e_11 = 0
    contiguity_ok = True
    rowcount_mismatches = []
    expected_tick = int(job["global_start_tick"])
    expected_cell = int(job["global_start_cell"])

    for fi, (fname, expected_rows) in enumerate(zip(files, file_rows), start=1):
        fp = os.path.join(data_dir, fname)
        t_file = time.time()
        log(f"[{fi}/{len(files)}] reading {fname} (expect {expected_rows:,} rows)")
        df = pd.read_csv(fp, usecols=["epoch_ms", "bid", "ask"],
                         dtype={"epoch_ms": "int64", "bid": "float64", "ask": "float64"})
        actual_rows = len(df)
        if actual_rows != expected_rows:
            msg = f"{fname}: actual rows {actual_rows:,} != manifest {expected_rows:,}; using actual"
            log("ROWCOUNT WARNING: " + msg)
            rowcount_mismatches.append({
                "file": fname, "actual": int(actual_rows), "manifest": int(expected_rows)
            })

        prices = ((df["bid"].to_numpy(np.float64) + df["ask"].to_numpy(np.float64)) / 2.0)
        times = df["epoch_ms"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        del df
        gc.collect()

        if len(carry_prices) > 0:
            prices = np.concatenate([carry_prices, prices])
            times = np.concatenate([carry_times, times])
            carry_prices = np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
            carry_times = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)

        n_complete = len(prices) // CELL_SIZE
        n_used = n_complete * CELL_SIZE
        if n_complete == 0:
            carry_prices = prices.copy()
            carry_times = times.copy()
            log(f"    no complete cell yet; carry={len(carry_prices)}")
            continue

        cell_prices = prices[:n_used].reshape(n_complete, CELL_SIZE)
        cell_times = times[:n_used].reshape(n_complete, CELL_SIZE)
        carry_prices = prices[n_used:].copy()
        carry_times = times[n_used:].copy()

        ft = compute_epicyclic_fourier_transform(cell_prices, K=K)
        if k_indices_ref is None:
            k_indices_ref = ft["k_indices"]

        cell_ids = np.arange(global_cell_id, global_cell_id + n_complete, dtype=np.int64)
        start_ticks = global_tick_cursor + np.arange(n_complete, dtype=np.int64) * CELL_SIZE
        end_ticks = start_ticks + (CELL_SIZE - 1)

        if int(start_ticks[0]) != expected_tick or int(cell_ids[0]) != expected_cell:
            contiguity_ok = False
            log(f"    CONTIGUITY GAP: expected tick {expected_tick}, cell {expected_cell}; "
                f"got tick {int(start_ticks[0])}, cell {int(cell_ids[0])}")
        if n_complete > 1 and not np.all(start_ticks[1:] == end_ticks[:-1] + 1):
            contiguity_ok = False
            log("    CONTIGUITY GAP inside chunk")

        chunk = pd.DataFrame({
            "cell_id": cell_ids,
            "cell_label": [f"Cell_{c}" for c in cell_ids],
            "tick_start_idx": start_ticks,
            "tick_end_idx": end_ticks,
            "time_open_ms": cell_times[:, 0],
            "time_close_ms": cell_times[:, -1],
            "p_open": ft["p_open"],
            "p_high": ft["p_high"],
            "p_low": ft["p_low"],
            "p_close": ft["p_close"],
            "p_mean": ft["p_mean"],
            "p_std": ft["p_std"],
            "y_open_norm": ft["y_open_norm"],
            "bridge_slope": ft["bridge_slope"],
            "rmse_residual": ft["rmse_residual"],
            "max_tick_error": ft["max_tick_error"],
            "norm_rmse": ft["norm_rmse"],
            "source_file": fname,
            "price_source": "mid(bid,ask)",
        })

        chunk_catalog_name = f"chunk_{node}_{fi:02d}_catalog.csv"
        chunk_npz_name = f"chunk_{node}_{fi:02d}_functions.npz"
        chunk_catalog_path = os.path.join(out_dir, chunk_catalog_name)
        chunk_npz_path = os.path.join(out_dir, chunk_npz_name)

        log(f"    writing {chunk_catalog_name} and {chunk_npz_name}")
        chunk.to_csv(chunk_catalog_path, index=False)
        np.savez_compressed(
            chunk_npz_path,
            cell_id=cell_ids,
            raw_tick_cells=cell_prices.astype(np.float64),
            c_k=ft["c_k"],
            k_indices=ft["k_indices"],
            p_mean=ft["p_mean"],
            p_std=ft["p_std"],
            y_open_norm=ft["y_open_norm"],
            bridge_slope=ft["bridge_slope"],
        )

        max_err = float(np.max(ft["max_tick_error"]))
        max_norm = float(np.max(ft["norm_rmse"]))
        under = int(np.sum(ft["max_tick_error"] < 1e-11))
        max_tick_error_all = max(max_tick_error_all, max_err)
        max_norm_rmse_all = max(max_norm_rmse_all, max_norm)
        cells_under_1e_11 += under

        chunks.append({
            "file": fname,
            "catalog": chunk_catalog_name,
            "npz": chunk_npz_name,
            "cells": int(n_complete),
            "ticks": int(n_used),
            "first_cell_id": int(cell_ids[0]),
            "last_cell_id": int(cell_ids[-1]),
            "first_tick_idx": int(start_ticks[0]),
            "last_tick_idx": int(end_ticks[-1]),
            "max_tick_error": max_err,
            "max_norm_rmse": max_norm,
            "cells_under_1e_11": under,
            "catalog_bytes": os.path.getsize(chunk_catalog_path),
            "npz_bytes": os.path.getsize(chunk_npz_path),
        })

        total_cells += n_complete
        total_ticks += n_used
        global_cell_id += n_complete
        global_tick_cursor += n_used
        expected_tick = int(end_ticks[-1]) + 1
        expected_cell = int(cell_ids[-1]) + 1

        log(f"    built {n_complete:,} cells | Cell_{cell_ids[0]}..Cell_{cell_ids[-1]} | "
            f"max_err={max_err:.3e} | carry={len(carry_prices)} | {time.time()-t_file:.1f}s")

        if do_upload and upload_url:
            for p in (chunk_catalog_path, chunk_npz_path):
                log(f"    uploading {os.path.basename(p)} ({os.path.getsize(p):,} bytes)")
                upload_file(p, upload_url + os.path.basename(p))

        progress_update(status="running", cells_done=total_cells, ticks_done=total_ticks,
                        files_done=fi, last_file=fname,
                        last_cell=int(cell_ids[-1]), max_tick_error=max_err,
                        chunks_done=len(chunks), rowcount_mismatches=rowcount_mismatches)

        del ft, chunk, cell_prices, cell_times, prices, times
        gc.collect()

    if total_cells == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("no complete cells built")
    if k_indices_ref is None:
        raise RuntimeError("no harmonic indices captured")

    lossless_ok = max_tick_error_all < 1e-9 and max_norm_rmse_all < 1e-12
    status = "ok" if (contiguity_ok and lossless_ok and not rowcount_mismatches) else (
        "rowcount_mismatch" if (contiguity_ok and lossless_ok and rowcount_mismatches)
        else "verification_failed"
    )

    summary = {
        "node": node,
        "status": status,
        "harmonics_K": K,
        "cells": int(total_cells),
        "ticks": int(total_ticks),
        "first_cell_id": int(chunks[0]["first_cell_id"]),
        "last_cell_id": int(chunks[-1]["last_cell_id"]),
        "first_tick_idx": int(chunks[0]["first_tick_idx"]),
        "last_tick_idx": int(chunks[-1]["last_tick_idx"]),
        "carry_ticks_left": int(len(carry_prices)),
        "contiguity_ok": bool(contiguity_ok),
        "lossless_ok": bool(lossless_ok),
        "max_tick_error": float(max_tick_error_all),
        "max_norm_rmse": float(max_norm_rmse_all),
        "cells_under_1e_11": int(cells_under_1e_11),
        "elapsed_sec": round(time.time() - run_started, 2),
        "files": files,
        "price_source": "mid(bid,ask)",
        "rowcount_mismatches": rowcount_mismatches,
        "chunks": chunks,
    }

    summary_path = os.path.join(out_dir, f"summary_{node}.json")
    json_dump(summary_path, summary)
    log("summary: " + json.dumps({k: summary[k] for k in (
        "status", "cells", "ticks", "first_cell_id", "last_cell_id",
        "contiguity_ok", "lossless_ok", "max_tick_error", "max_norm_rmse",
        "cells_under_1e_11", "elapsed_sec")}))
    log(f"wrote {summary_path} with {len(chunks)} chunks")

    if do_upload and upload_url:
        progress_update(status="uploading", summary=summary,
                        cells_done=total_cells, ticks_done=total_ticks,
                        files_done=len(files))
        log(f"uploading summary_{node}.json")
        upload_file(summary_path, upload_url + os.path.basename(summary_path))
        upload_json(progress, f"progress_{node}.json", upload_url)

    if not (contiguity_ok and lossless_ok):
        log("VERIFICATION FAILED")
        return 1
    if rowcount_mismatches:
        log("COMPLETE WITH ROWCOUNT WARNINGS")
        return 0
    log("PHASE 1 WORKER COMPLETE")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        sys.exit(main())
    except Exception:
        traceback.print_exc()
        sys.exit(1)

The worker is the only place where the 150-tick cell boundary is defined. It reads raw ticks, validates continuity, writes the cell record and computes the 75-harmonic epicycle encoding. Every downstream phase depends on the invariant it establishes: cell n+1 starts exactly one tick after cell n ends.

phase1/scripts/phase1_merge.py - node merge, seam audit, catalog assembly (300 lines, 11.6 KB)
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import shutil
import zipfile
import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from numpy.lib.format import open_memmap, write_array_header_1_0, dtype_to_descr


def log(msg):
    print(f"[{time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')}] {msg}", flush=True)


def load_chunk_index(out_dir):
    rows = []
    for name in sorted(os.listdir(out_dir)):
        if not (name.startswith("chunk_") and name.endswith("_catalog.csv")):
            continue
        path = os.path.join(out_dir, name)
        df = pd.read_csv(path, usecols=["cell_id", "tick_start_idx", "tick_end_idx"])
        if len(df) == 0:
            continue
        npz = path[:-len("_catalog.csv")] + "_functions.npz"
        if not os.path.isfile(npz):
            raise FileNotFoundError(npz)
        rows.append({
            "catalog": path,
            "npz": npz,
            "cells": int(len(df)),
            "first_cell": int(df["cell_id"].iloc[0]),
            "last_cell": int(df["cell_id"].iloc[-1]),
            "first_tick": int(df["tick_start_idx"].iloc[0]),
            "last_tick": int(df["tick_end_idx"].iloc[-1]),
        })
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r["first_cell"])
    return rows


def verify_chunk_chain(rows):
    problems = []
    for i, r in enumerate(rows):
        if r["last_cell"] - r["first_cell"] + 1 != r["cells"]:
            problems.append(f"cell count mismatch in {os.path.basename(r['catalog'])}")
        if r["last_tick"] - r["first_tick"] + 1 != r["cells"] * 150:
            problems.append(f"tick span mismatch in {os.path.basename(r['catalog'])}")
        if i > 0:
            p = rows[i - 1]
            if r["first_cell"] != p["last_cell"] + 1:
                problems.append(f"cell gap {p['last_cell']} -> {r['first_cell']}")
            if r["first_tick"] != p["last_tick"] + 1:
                problems.append(f"tick gap {p['last_tick']} -> {r['first_tick']}")
    return problems


def write_npz_streaming(npz_path, rows, catalog, temp_dir):
    os.makedirs(temp_dir, exist_ok=True)
    n = len(catalog)
    m = 150
    ck_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, "c_k.npy")
    raw_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, "raw_tick_cells.npy")
    rad_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, "radii.npy")
    pha_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, "phases.npy")

    ck_mm = open_memmap(ck_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.complex128, shape=(n, m))
    raw_mm = open_memmap(raw_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float64, shape=(n, m))
    rad_mm = open_memmap(rad_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float64, shape=(n, m))
    pha_mm = open_memmap(pha_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float64, shape=(n, m))

    k_indices_ref = None
    p_mean = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p_std = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y_open = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    slope = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cell_id = np.empty(n, dtype=np.int64)

    pos = 0
    for r in rows:
        with np.load(r["npz"], allow_pickle=False) as z:
            ids = z["cell_id"].astype(np.int64)
            if len(ids) != r["cells"]:
                raise ValueError(f"{r['npz']}: cell count {len(ids)} != {r['cells']}")
            if int(ids[0]) != r["first_cell"] or int(ids[-1]) != r["last_cell"]:
                raise ValueError(f"{r['npz']}: cell id range mismatch")
            sl = slice(pos, pos + len(ids))
            ck = z["c_k"]
            raw = z["raw_tick_cells"]
            if ck.shape != (len(ids), m) or raw.shape != (len(ids), m):
                raise ValueError(f"{r['npz']}: bad shapes ck={ck.shape} raw={raw.shape}")
            ck_mm[sl] = ck
            raw_mm[sl] = raw
            rad_mm[sl] = np.abs(ck)
            pha_mm[sl] = np.angle(ck)
            cell_id[sl] = ids
            p_mean[sl] = z["p_mean"]
            p_std[sl] = z["p_std"]
            y_open[sl] = z["y_open_norm"]
            slope[sl] = z["bridge_slope"]
            if k_indices_ref is None:
                k_indices_ref = z["k_indices"].astype(np.int64)
            pos += len(ids)
        log(f"  streamed {os.path.basename(r['npz'])} ({len(ids):,} cells)")

    if pos != n:
        raise ValueError(f"streamed {pos} cells, expected {n}")
    if k_indices_ref is None:
        raise ValueError("no k_indices found")
    if not np.array_equal(cell_id, catalog["cell_id"].to_numpy(np.int64)):
        raise ValueError("npz cell_id order does not match catalog order")

    for mm in (ck_mm, raw_mm, rad_mm, pha_mm):
        mm.flush()
    del ck_mm, raw_mm, rad_mm, pha_mm

    log(f"packing {npz_path}")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(npz_path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as zf:
        zf.write(ck_path, arcname="c_k.npy")
        zf.write(raw_path, arcname="raw_tick_cells.npy")
        zf.write(rad_path, arcname="radii.npy")
        zf.write(pha_path, arcname="phases.npy")

        for name, arr in (
            ("cell_id", cell_id),
            ("k_indices", k_indices_ref),
            ("p_mean", p_mean),
            ("p_std", p_std),
            ("y_open_norm", y_open),
            ("bridge_slope", slope),
        ):
            member = name + ".npy"
            with zf.open(member, "w", force_zip64=True) as fp:
                write_array_header_1_0(fp, {
                    "shape": arr.shape,
                    "fortran_order": False,
                    "descr": dtype_to_descr(arr.dtype),
                })
                fp.write(arr.tobytes(order="C"))

    for p in (ck_path, raw_path, rad_path, pha_path):
        try:
            os.remove(p)
        except OSError:
            pass


def write_excel(path, catalog, rows, n_rows):
    rows = list(rows)
    pieces = []
    raw_pieces = []
    got = 0
    for r in rows:
        take = min(r["cells"], n_rows - got)
        if take <= 0:
            break
        with np.load(r["npz"], allow_pickle=False) as z:
            pieces.append(z["c_k"][:take])
            raw_pieces.append(z["raw_tick_cells"][:take])
        got += take
    if got == 0:
        raise ValueError("no rows for Excel export")

    ck = np.vstack(pieces)
    raw = np.vstack(raw_pieces)
    meta = catalog.iloc[:got].copy()

    df_ticks = pd.DataFrame(raw, columns=[f"Tick_{i+1}" for i in range(raw.shape[1])])
    df_ticks.insert(0, "cell_label", meta["cell_label"].values)

    k_idx = np.load(rows[0]["npz"], allow_pickle=False)["k_indices"].astype(int)
    fn = {}
    for j, k in enumerate(k_idx):
        tag = "0" if k == 0 else (f"p{k}" if k > 0 else f"m{abs(k)}")
        fn[f"R_{tag}"] = np.abs(ck[:, j])
        fn[f"Phi_{tag}"] = np.angle(ck[:, j])
    df_fn = pd.DataFrame(fn)
    df_fn.insert(0, "cell_label", meta["cell_label"].values)

    log(f"writing Excel {path} ({got:,} rows, {df_fn.shape[1]} function columns)")
    with pd.ExcelWriter(path, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
        meta.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="Cell_Catalog", index=False)
        df_fn.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="Epicycle_Math_Functions", index=False)
        df_ticks.to_excel(writer, sheet_name="Raw_150_Tick_Cells", index=False)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=r"C:\cluster\phase1\out")
    ap.add_argument("--final", default=r"C:\cluster\phase1\final")
    ap.add_argument("--excel-rows", type=int, default=10000)
    ap.add_argument("--skip-excel", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--expected-first-cell", type=int, default=1)
    ap.add_argument("--expected-last-cell", type=int, default=591171)
    ap.add_argument("--expected-cells", type=int, default=591171)
    ap.add_argument("--skip-boundaries", action="store_true")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    os.makedirs(args.final, exist_ok=True)
    temp_dir = os.path.join(args.final, "_tmp_npy")
    started = time.time()

    rows = load_chunk_index(args.out)
    if not rows:
        raise RuntimeError(f"no chunks found in {args.out}")
    log(f"found {len(rows)} chunks, {sum(r['cells'] for r in rows):,} cells")
    problems = verify_chunk_chain(rows)
    if problems:
        for p in problems:
            log("CHAIN PROBLEM: " + p)
        raise RuntimeError(f"{len(problems)} chunk chain problems")

    catalog_parts = []
    for r in rows:
        catalog_parts.append(pd.read_csv(r["catalog"]))
    catalog = pd.concat(catalog_parts, ignore_index=True)
    catalog = catalog.sort_values("cell_id", kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)
    del catalog_parts

    ids = catalog["cell_id"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    starts = catalog["tick_start_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    ends = catalog["tick_end_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    if ids[0] != args.expected_first_cell:
        raise ValueError(f"first cell {ids[0]} != {args.expected_first_cell}")
    if ids[-1] != args.expected_last_cell:
        raise ValueError(f"last cell {ids[-1]} != {args.expected_last_cell}")
    if len(catalog) != args.expected_cells:
        raise ValueError(f"cell count {len(catalog)} != {args.expected_cells}")
    if not np.all(ids[1:] == ids[:-1] + 1):
        raise ValueError("catalog cell ids are not contiguous")
    if not np.all(starts[1:] == ends[:-1] + 1):
        raise ValueError("catalog tick indices are not contiguous")
    expected_first_tick = (args.expected_first_cell - 1) * 150 + 1
    if starts[0] != expected_first_tick:
        raise ValueError(f"first tick {starts[0]} != {expected_first_tick}")

    expected_last_tick = int(ids[-1]) * 150
    tail = int(catalog["tick_end_idx"].iloc[-1])
    if tail != expected_last_tick:
        raise ValueError(f"last tick {tail} != {expected_last_tick}")
    log(f"verified catalog: Cell_{ids[0]}..Cell_{ids[-1]}, ticks {starts[0]}..{tail}")

    parquet_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_cell_catalog.parquet")
    log(f"writing {parquet_path}")
    catalog.to_parquet(parquet_path, index=False, engine="fastparquet")

    npz_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz")
    write_npz_streaming(npz_path, rows, catalog, temp_dir)

    excel_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_inspection.xlsx")
    if not args.skip_excel:
        write_excel(excel_path, catalog, rows, args.excel_rows)

    report = {
        "status": "ok",
        "cells": int(len(catalog)),
        "ticks": int(tail),
        "first_cell_id": int(ids[0]),
        "last_cell_id": int(ids[-1]),
        "first_tick_idx": int(starts[0]),
        "last_tick_idx": int(tail),
        "chunks": len(rows),
        "max_tick_error": float(catalog["max_tick_error"].max()),
        "max_norm_rmse": float(catalog["norm_rmse"].max()),
        "cells_under_1e_11": int((catalog["max_tick_error"] < 1e-11).sum()),
        "contiguity_ok": True,
        "node_boundaries": [],
        "outputs": {
            "catalog_parquet": parquet_path,
            "functions_npz": npz_path,
            "inspection_xlsx": excel_path if not args.skip_excel else None,
        },
        "elapsed_sec": round(time.time() - started, 2),
    }

    if not args.skip_boundaries:
        for cid in (171621, 381378):
            row = catalog.loc[catalog["cell_id"] == cid]
            nxt = catalog.loc[catalog["cell_id"] == cid + 1]
            if len(row) and len(nxt):
                report["node_boundaries"].append({
                    "left_cell": int(cid),
                    "left_last_tick": int(row["tick_end_idx"].iloc[0]),
                    "right_cell": int(cid + 1),
                    "right_first_tick": int(nxt["tick_start_idx"].iloc[0]),
                    "ok": bool(int(nxt["tick_start_idx"].iloc[0]) == int(row["tick_end_idx"].iloc[0]) + 1),
                })

    report_path = os.path.join(args.final, "phase1_verification.json")
    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
    log("verification: " + json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    log("PHASE 1 MERGE COMPLETE")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

The merger is also the seam auditor. The two internal node boundaries (cells 171,621|171,622 and 381,378|381,379) are checked for tick continuity explicitly, because a corrupted seam would silently create a fake 150-tick "anomaly" at two points in the corpus and nothing downstream would notice.

phase1/scripts/verify_phase1.py - independent reconstruction verifier (118 lines, 4.9 KB)
﻿import argparse
import json
import os
import zipfile

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from openpyxl import load_workbook


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--final", default=r"C:\cluster\phase1\final")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    npz_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz")
    parquet_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_cell_catalog.parquet")
    xlsx_path = os.path.join(args.final, "jump10_phase1_inspection.xlsx")

    report = {"status": "ok", "checks": {}}

    with zipfile.ZipFile(npz_path) as zf:
        bad = zf.testzip()
    report["checks"]["npz_crc_ok"] = bad is None
    report["checks"]["npz_crc_bad_member"] = bad

    catalog = pd.read_parquet(parquet_path, engine="fastparquet")
    n = len(catalog)
    expected = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    first = catalog["tick_start_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    last = catalog["tick_end_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    report["checks"]["catalog_rows"] = n
    report["checks"]["catalog_columns"] = int(catalog.shape[1])
    report["checks"]["cell_ids_contiguous"] = bool(np.array_equal(catalog["cell_id"].to_numpy(np.int64), expected))
    report["checks"]["cell_labels_match"] = bool(
        np.array_equal(catalog["cell_label"].astype(str).to_numpy(), np.char.add("Cell_", expected.astype(str)))
    )
    report["checks"]["tick_ranges_contiguous"] = bool(
        np.all(first == 1 + 150 * np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64))
        and np.all(last == 150 * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.int64))
    )
    report["checks"]["catalog_first_tick"] = int(first[0])
    report["checks"]["catalog_last_tick"] = int(last[-1])
    numeric = catalog.select_dtypes(include=[np.number])
    report["checks"]["catalog_all_finite"] = bool(np.isfinite(numeric.to_numpy()).all())

    sample = np.array(
        sorted(set([0, 1, 13505, 171620, 171621, 210000, 381377, 381378, 400000, 591169, 591170]))
    )
    with np.load(npz_path, allow_pickle=False) as z:
        ids = z["cell_id"][sample]
        ck = z["c_k"][sample]
        raw = z["raw_tick_cells"][sample]
        radii = z["radii"][sample]
        phases = z["phases"][sample]
        p_mean = z["p_mean"][sample]
        p_std = z["p_std"][sample]
        y_open = z["y_open_norm"][sample]
        slope = z["bridge_slope"][sample]
        k_idx = z["k_indices"].astype(np.int64)
        report["checks"]["npz_cell_id_count"] = int(z["cell_id"].shape[0])
        report["checks"]["npz_cell_id_ok"] = bool(np.array_equal(z["cell_id"].astype(np.int64), expected))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 150, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    z_rec = np.fft.ifft(ck * 150.0, axis=1)
    y_norm_rec = z_rec.imag + y_open[:, None] + t * slope[:, None]
    prices_rec = y_norm_rec * p_std[:, None] + p_mean[:, None]
    err = np.abs(prices_rec - raw)
    report["checks"]["sample_rows"] = sample.tolist()
    report["checks"]["sample_cell_ids"] = ids.astype(int).tolist()
    report["checks"]["sample_max_abs_error"] = float(err.max())
    report["checks"]["sample_max_norm_rmse"] = float(
        np.sqrt(np.mean((prices_rec - raw) ** 2 / (p_std[:, None] ** 2), axis=1)).max()
    )
    report["checks"]["sample_all_finite"] = bool(
        np.isfinite(ck).all()
        and np.isfinite(raw).all()
        and np.isfinite(radii).all()
        and np.isfinite(phases).all()
        and np.isfinite(prices_rec).all()
    )
    report["checks"]["radii_match"] = bool(np.allclose(radii, np.abs(ck), rtol=0.0, atol=0.0))
    report["checks"]["phases_match"] = bool(np.allclose(phases, np.angle(ck), rtol=0.0, atol=0.0))
    report["checks"]["k_indices_ok"] = bool(np.array_equal(k_idx, np.fft.fftfreq(150, d=1.0 / 150).astype(np.int64)))
    report["checks"]["harmonics_count"] = int(len(k_idx))
    report["checks"]["lossless_ok"] = bool(err.max() < 1e-11)

    wb = load_workbook(xlsx_path, read_only=True)
    for name in ("Cell_Catalog", "Epicycle_Math_Functions", "Raw_150_Tick_Cells"):
        ws = wb[name]
        report["checks"][f"xlsx_{name}"] = [int(ws.max_row), int(ws.max_column)]
    wb.close()

    report["outputs"] = {
        "catalog_parquet_bytes": os.path.getsize(parquet_path),
        "functions_npz_bytes": os.path.getsize(npz_path),
        "inspection_xlsx_bytes": os.path.getsize(xlsx_path),
    }
    report["checks"]["all_ok"] = bool(
        report["checks"]["npz_crc_ok"]
        and report["checks"]["cell_ids_contiguous"]
        and report["checks"]["cell_labels_match"]
        and report["checks"]["tick_ranges_contiguous"]
        and report["checks"]["catalog_all_finite"]
        and report["checks"]["npz_cell_id_ok"]
        and report["checks"]["sample_all_finite"]
        and report["checks"]["radii_match"]
        and report["checks"]["phases_match"]
        and report["checks"]["k_indices_ok"]
        and report["checks"]["lossless_ok"]
        and report["checks"]["xlsx_Cell_Catalog"] == [10001, int(catalog.shape[1] + 0)]
    )
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

A.2 Phase 2 - relational graph, vault and models

phase2/pqcompat.py - parquet shim (duckdb -> pyarrow -> fastparquet) (171 lines, 5.1 KB)
"""
Parquet compatibility shim for this machine.

Situation (2026-10-27):
  * pyarrow's _compute.pyd is blocked by an Application Control policy.
  * fastparquet's cencoding.pyd is now blocked too (it worked for a while).
  * duckdb is NOT blocked and ships its own parquet reader/writer -> preferred.

Order of preference: duckdb -> pyarrow -> fastparquet (with the pure-python
cramjam stub so UNCOMPRESSED parquet still works where duckdb is missing).
"""
import sys
import os
import gzip
import types
import sqlite3


def _install_stub():
    if "cramjam" in sys.modules:
        return
    mod = types.ModuleType("cramjam")

    class Buffer(bytearray):
        pass

    def _unsupported(name):
        def _f(*a, **k):
            raise NotImplementedError(
                f"cramjam.{name} unavailable: the cramjam DLL is blocked by "
                f"Application Control on this host. Write parquet with compression=None."
            )
        return _f

    class _Gzip:
        @staticmethod
        def compress(data, level=6, **kw):
            return gzip.compress(bytes(data), compresslevel=int(level or 6))

        @staticmethod
        def decompress(data, output_len=None, **kw):
            return gzip.decompress(bytes(data))

        @staticmethod
        def decompress_into(data, out):
            b = gzip.decompress(bytes(data))
            mv = memoryview(out)
            mv[:len(b)] = b
            return len(b)

    class _Snappy:
        compress_raw = staticmethod(_unsupported("snappy.compress_raw"))
        decompress_raw = staticmethod(_unsupported("snappy.decompress_raw"))
        decompress_raw_into = staticmethod(_unsupported("snappy.decompress_raw_into"))
        compress = staticmethod(_unsupported("snappy.compress"))
        decompress = staticmethod(_unsupported("snappy.decompress"))

    class _Brotli:
        compress = staticmethod(_unsupported("brotli.compress"))
        decompress = staticmethod(_unsupported("brotli.decompress"))
        decompress_into = staticmethod(_unsupported("brotli.decompress_into"))

    class _Lz4:
        compress = staticmethod(_unsupported("lz4.compress"))
        decompress = staticmethod(_unsupported("lz4.decompress"))
        compress_block = staticmethod(_unsupported("lz4.compress_block"))
        decompress_block = staticmethod(_unsupported("lz4.decompress_block"))

    class _Zstd:
        compress = staticmethod(_unsupported("zstd.compress"))
        decompress = staticmethod(_unsupported("zstd.decompress"))
        decompress_into = staticmethod(_unsupported("zstd.decompress_into"))

    mod.Buffer = Buffer
    mod.gzip = _Gzip
    mod.snappy = _Snappy
    mod.brotli = _Brotli
    mod.lz4 = _Lz4
    mod.zstd = _Zstd
    sys.modules["cramjam"] = mod


def _duckdb():
    import duckdb
    return duckdb


def import_fastparquet():
    try:
        import fastparquet  # noqa: F401
        return fastparquet
    except ImportError as e:
        if "cramjam" not in str(e):
            raise
        _install_stub()
        import fastparquet  # noqa: F401
        return fastparquet


def engine():
    """'duckdb' | 'pyarrow' | 'fastparquet' | None"""
    try:
        _duckdb()
        return "duckdb"
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import pyarrow  # noqa: F401
        return "pyarrow"
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        import_fastparquet()
        return "fastparquet"
    except Exception:
        return None


def _q(path):
    return "'" + str(path).replace("'", "''") + "'"


def write_parquet(df, path):
    path = os.path.abspath(str(path))
    if os.path.exists(path):
        os.remove(path)
    eng = engine()
    if eng == "duckdb":
        con = _duckdb().connect()
        try:
            con.register("_df", df)
            con.execute(f"COPY _df TO {_q(path)} (FORMAT PARQUET, COMPRESSION SNAPPY)")
        finally:
            con.close()
    elif eng == "pyarrow":
        df.to_parquet(path, index=False, engine="pyarrow")
    elif eng == "fastparquet":
        df.to_parquet(path, index=False, engine="fastparquet", compression=None)
    else:
        raise RuntimeError("no usable parquet engine (pyarrow/fastparquet blocked, duckdb missing)")
    return eng


def read_parquet(path):
    path = os.path.abspath(str(path))
    eng = engine()
    if eng == "duckdb":
        con = _duckdb().connect()
        try:
            return con.execute(f"SELECT * FROM read_parquet({_q(path)})").df()
        finally:
            con.close()
    if eng == "pyarrow":
        import pandas as pd
        return pd.read_parquet(path, engine="pyarrow")
    if eng == "fastparquet":
        import pandas as pd
        return pd.read_parquet(path, engine="fastparquet")
    raise RuntimeError("no usable parquet engine")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import pandas as pd
    import numpy as np
    print("engine:", engine())
    df = pd.DataFrame({"a": np.arange(10), "b": np.linspace(0, 1, 10), "c": list("abcdefghij")})
    p = r"C:\cluster\phase2\_pq_test.parquet"
    print("write ->", write_parquet(df, p))
    back = read_parquet(p)
    print(back.head(3).to_string())
    print("roundtrip ok:", back.shape == df.shape)

This 143-line file is why the project runs at all on the master machine. Application Control blocks PyArrow's native modules and fastparquet's cencoding.pyd; duckdb is the only working engine there, and it is absent on Kali. Every other script imports this shim instead of pandas' parquet readers.

phase2/phase2_step1_zone_and_covariant_db.py - quartile cut, descriptor bank, hub selection (335 lines, 14.0 KB)
"""
PHASE 2 - STEP 1 (v2)
Structural zone decomposer + distributed relational covariant scanner.

v2 fixes (post phase2_mathematicalfix.md):
  * reads the repaired/whitened cache with mmap (never loads 2.6 GB into RAM)
  * top-K capped peer evaluation  -> O(N*D) GEMM + O(K*D) per row, no more
    materialising 500k-element peer sets or R_mat[peers] copies
  * growable numpy edge buffers instead of multi-million python tuples
  * parquet via pyarrow when available, else fastparquet
  * periodic checkpointing + ETA so a long shard can be resumed/monitored
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import argparse

for _v in ("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "MKL_NUM_THREADS", "OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS",
           "NUMEXPR_NUM_THREADS", "VECLIB_MAXIMUM_THREADS", "BLIS_NUM_THREADS"):
    os.environ.setdefault(_v, "8")

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

FIB_TICKS = [35, 57, 75, 93, 118]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# parquet helper (pyarrow is blocked by Application Control on this host)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
import pqcompat

ENGINE = pqcompat.engine()


def write_parquet(df, path):
    if ENGINE is not None:
        pqcompat.write_parquet(df, path)
    else:
        df.to_csv(path.replace(".parquet", ".csv"), index=False)
    return ENGINE


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# zone extraction (kept for provenance / cold start; not used when cache exists)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def zone_whitened_fft(y_slice, k_max=12):
    L = y_slice.shape[1]
    if L < 4:
        return np.zeros((y_slice.shape[0], max(1, k_max * 2 + 3)), dtype=np.float32)
    t_lin = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L, dtype=np.float32)[None, :]
    slope = y_slice[:, -1:] - y_slice[:, 0:1]
    bridge = y_slice - (y_slice[:, 0:1] + t_lin * slope)
    fft_c = np.fft.rfft(bridge, axis=1)[:, 1:k_max + 1] / max(L, 1)
    k_weights = np.arange(1, fft_c.shape[1] + 1, dtype=np.float32)[None, :]
    fft_w = fft_c * k_weights
    vec = np.hstack([fft_w.real, fft_w.imag, slope.astype(np.float32)]).astype(np.float32)
    norms = np.linalg.norm(vec, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    norms = np.where(norms < 1e-9, 1.0, norms)
    return vec / norms


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# distributed relational covariant scan
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def _boundaries(N, weights):
    b = [0]
    s = 0.0
    for w in weights:
        s += w
        b.append(int(round(N * s)))
    b[-1] = N
    return b


class EdgeBuffer:
    """Growable (n,4) float32 edge store - no multi-million python tuples."""

    def __init__(self, cap_hint=1_000_000):
        self.blocks = []
        self.n = 0
        self.block = np.empty((cap_hint, 4), dtype=np.float32)
        self.fill = 0

    def add(self, src, dst, cov, lock):
        if self.fill >= self.block.shape[0]:
            self.blocks.append(self.block[:self.fill])
            self.block = np.empty((max(65536, self.block.shape[0]), 4), dtype=np.float32)
            self.fill = 0
        self.block[self.fill] = (src, dst, cov, lock)
        self.fill += 1
        self.n += 1

    def array(self):
        parts = self.blocks + [self.block[:self.fill]]
        if not parts:
            return np.empty((0, 4), dtype=np.float32)
        return np.vstack(parts)


def scan_relational_covariant_shard(
    cache_path,
    node_id=0,
    num_nodes=3,
    sim_threshold=0.85,
    min_covariant_peers=12,
    chunk_size=256,
    top_peers=512,
    max_edges=24,
    output_dir="C:/cluster/phase2/shards",
    checkpoint_secs=120,
    max_rows=None,
    row_start_override=None,
    row_end_override=None,
):
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    z = np.load(cache_path, mmap_mode="r")
    keys = set(z.files)

    if "trigger_vec_dense" in keys:
        T_mat = z["trigger_vec_dense"]
        R_mat = z["resolution_vec_dense"]
        mode = "dense(whitened)"
    else:
        T_mat = z["trigger_vec"]
        R_mat = z["resolution_vec"]
        mode = "fft(whitened)"

    N, D = T_mat.shape
    held_all = z["protected_held"]
    pol_all = z["ext_polarity"]
    ret_all = z["q4_net_return"]
    tick_all = z["ext_tick"]
    bico_key = "phase_closure_abs_cos" if "phase_closure_abs_cos" in keys else "bicoherence"
    bico_all = z[bico_key]

    if row_start_override is not None and row_end_override is not None:
        row_start, row_end = int(row_start_override), int(row_end_override)
    else:
        weights = [0.40, 0.30, 0.30] if num_nodes == 3 else [1.0 / num_nodes] * num_nodes
        bounds = _boundaries(N, weights)
        row_start, row_end = bounds[node_id], bounds[node_id + 1]
    if max_rows:
        row_end = min(row_end, row_start + max_rows)

    print(f"[*] Node {node_id}/{num_nodes}: rows {row_start:,}..{row_end:,} of {N:,} "
          f"| T={T_mat.shape} D={D} | thr={sim_threshold} min_peers={min_covariant_peers} "
          f"top_peers={top_peers} chunk={chunk_size} | mode={mode} | engine={ENGINE}", flush=True)

    T_T = np.asarray(T_mat).T.copy()  # (D,N) contiguous for fast GEMM
    node_rows = []   # list of tuples (compact)
    edges = EdgeBuffer()

    n_pass = 0
    n_scanned = 0
    t_start = time.time()
    t_ckpt = time.time()
    stats = {"max_abs_r": 0.0, "max_abs_r_cell": 0, "max_lock": -2.0, "max_lock_cell": 0,
             "max_abs_r_edge": 0.0, "edges": 0, "anomalies": 0}
    n_chunks = (row_end - row_start + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size
    chunk_i = 0

    def checkpoint(tag="partial"):
        if not node_rows:
            return
        cols = ["anchor_cell_id", "covariant_peer_count", "peers_evaluated",
                "pos_corr_peers", "neg_mirror_peers", "mean_0_50_similarity",
                "max_0_50_similarity", "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "frac_75_100_exact_lock",
                "protected_ext_win_rate", "directional_50_100_win_rate",
                "direction_consistency", "anomaly_z_score", "bicoherence_phase_lock",
                "target_ext_tick_median", "recommended_action"]
        dfn = pd.DataFrame.from_records(node_rows, columns=cols)
        write_parquet(dfn, os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_anomalies_node{node_id}_{tag}.parquet"))
        e = edges.array()
        dfe = pd.DataFrame({"src_idx": e[:, 0].astype(np.int64),
                            "dst_idx": e[:, 1].astype(np.int64),
                            "cov_0_50": e[:, 2], "lock_50_100": e[:, 3]})
        write_parquet(dfe, os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_edges_node{node_id}_{tag}.parquet"))
        with open(os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_stats_node{node_id}_{tag}.json"), "w") as f:
            json.dump({"node": node_id, "rows_done": n_scanned, "row_end": row_end,
                       "anomalies": n_pass, "source": mode, "threshold": sim_threshold,
                       **stats}, f, indent=2)

    for i0 in range(row_start, row_end, chunk_size):
        i1 = min(i0 + chunk_size, row_end)
        Q = np.asarray(T_mat[i0:i1], dtype=np.float32)
        sim = Q @ T_T  # (rows_in_chunk, N) float32

        for lr in range(i1 - i0):
            g = i0 + lr
            row = sim[lr]
            lo, hi = max(0, g - 2), min(N, g + 3)
            row[lo:hi] = 0.0
            absr = np.abs(row)
            cand = np.flatnonzero(absr >= sim_threshold)
            n_cand = int(cand.size)
            if n_cand < min_covariant_peers:
                continue

            if n_cand > top_peers:
                part = cand[np.argpartition(absr[cand], -top_peers)[-top_peers:]]
                sel = part[np.argsort(absr[part])[::-1]]
            else:
                sel = cand[np.argsort(absr[cand])[::-1]]
            k = sel.size

            signed = row[sel]
            pol = np.sign(signed)
            z_res = R_mat[g]
            y_res = np.asarray(R_mat[sel], dtype=np.float32) * pol[:, None]
            cont = y_res @ np.asarray(z_res, dtype=np.float32)  # (k,)

            mean_cont = float(cont.mean())
            frac_lock = float(np.mean(cont >= 0.65))
            p_out = np.asarray(held_all[sel])
            p_dirs = np.asarray(pol_all[sel], dtype=np.int32) * pol
            dir_cons = float(abs(p_dirs.mean()))
            prot_win = float(p_out.mean())
            aligned_ret = np.asarray(ret_all[sel]) * pol
            med_ret = float(np.median(aligned_ret))
            dir_win = float(np.mean(np.sign(aligned_ret + 1e-18) == np.sign(med_ret + 1e-18)))
            edge_eff = max(prot_win, dir_win * dir_cons)
            z_sc = (edge_eff - 0.50) * np.sqrt(k) / 0.50
            mean_sim = float(absr[sel].mean())
            max_sim = float(absr[sel].max())

            stats["max_abs_r"] = max(stats["max_abs_r"], max_sim)
            if max_sim >= stats["max_abs_r"]:
                stats["max_abs_r_cell"] = int(g + 1)
            if mean_cont > stats["max_lock"]:
                stats["max_lock"] = mean_cont
                stats["max_lock_cell"] = int(g + 1)

            if edge_eff >= 0.53 or mean_cont >= 0.30:
                node_rows.append((
                    int(g + 1), n_cand, int(k),
                    int(np.sum(pol > 0)), int(np.sum(pol < 0)),
                    round(mean_sim, 4), round(max_sim, 4),
                    round(mean_cont, 4), round(frac_lock, 4),
                    round(prot_win, 4), round(dir_win, 4), round(dir_cons, 4),
                    round(float(z_sc), 3), round(float(bico_all[g]), 4),
                    int(np.median(np.asarray(tick_all[sel]))),
                    "SELL_PROTECTED_HIGH" if float(p_dirs.mean()) < 0 else "BUY_PROTECTED_LOW",
                ))
                n_pass += 1
                for j in range(min(max_edges, k)):
                    src, dst = int(g), int(sel[j])
                    if src == dst:
                        continue
                    if src > dst:
                        src, dst = dst, src
                    lock = float(cont[j])
                    edges.add(src, dst, float(signed[j]), lock)
                    if abs(float(signed[j])) > stats["max_abs_r_edge"]:
                        stats["max_abs_r_edge"] = abs(float(signed[j]))

        n_scanned += (i1 - i0)
        chunk_i += 1
        now = time.time()
        if now - t_ckpt >= checkpoint_secs:
            stats["anomalies"] = n_pass
            stats["edges"] = edges.n
            checkpoint()
            t_ckpt = now

        if chunk_i % 5 == 0 or chunk_i == n_chunks:
            el = time.time() - t_start
            rate = n_scanned / max(el, 1e-9)
            eta = (row_end - i1) / max(rate, 1e-9)
            print(f"    -> node {node_id}: {i1:,}/{row_end:,} rows "
                  f"({100.0 * (i1 - row_start) / max(1, row_end - row_start):5.1f}%) "
                  f"| {rate:,.0f} rows/s | elapsed {el/60:.1f}m | eta {eta/60:.1f}m "
                  f"| anomalies {n_pass:,} | edges {edges.n:,}", flush=True)

    stats["anomalies"] = n_pass
    stats["edges"] = edges.n
    checkpoint("final")

    # compact final artifacts (also overwrite the unsuffixed names used downstream)
    cols = ["anchor_cell_id", "covariant_peer_count", "peers_evaluated",
            "pos_corr_peers", "neg_mirror_peers", "mean_0_50_similarity",
            "max_0_50_similarity", "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "frac_75_100_exact_lock",
            "protected_ext_win_rate", "directional_50_100_win_rate",
            "direction_consistency", "anomaly_z_score", "bicoherence_phase_lock",
            "target_ext_tick_median", "recommended_action"]
    dfn = pd.DataFrame.from_records(node_rows, columns=cols)
    e = edges.array()
    dfe = pd.DataFrame({"src_idx": e[:, 0].astype(np.int64),
                        "dst_idx": e[:, 1].astype(np.int64),
                        "cov_0_50": e[:, 2], "lock_50_100": e[:, 3]})
    write_parquet(dfn, os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_anomalies_node{node_id}.parquet"))
    write_parquet(dfe, os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_edges_node{node_id}.parquet"))
    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, f"covariant_stats_node{node_id}.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump({"node": node_id, "rows_scanned": n_scanned, "row_start": row_start,
                   "row_end": row_end, "anomalies": n_pass, "source": mode,
                   "threshold": sim_threshold, "top_peers": top_peers,
                   "minutes": round((time.time() - t_start) / 60.0, 2), **stats}, f, indent=2)
    print(f"[+] node {node_id} done: {n_pass:,} anomaly hubs, {edges.n:,} edges, "
          f"{(time.time() - t_start)/60:.1f} min -> {output_dir}", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--npz_path", type=str, default="C:/cluster/phase2/jump10_phase2_subzones.npz")
    p.add_argument("--out_dir", type=str, default="C:/cluster/phase2")
    p.add_argument("--node_id", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--num_nodes", type=int, default=3)
    p.add_argument("--sim_threshold", type=float, default=0.85)
    p.add_argument("--min_peers", type=int, default=12)
    p.add_argument("--top_peers", type=int, default=512)
    p.add_argument("--max_edges", type=int, default=24)
    p.add_argument("--chunk_size", type=int, default=256)
    p.add_argument("--checkpoint_secs", type=int, default=120)
    p.add_argument("--max_rows", type=int, default=0)
    p.add_argument("--row_start", type=int, default=-1)
    p.add_argument("--row_end", type=int, default=-1)
    a = p.parse_args()

    if not os.path.exists(a.npz_path):
        print(f"[-] cache not found: {a.npz_path}", flush=True)
        sys.exit(1)
    scan_relational_covariant_shard(
        a.npz_path,
        node_id=a.node_id, num_nodes=a.num_nodes,
        sim_threshold=a.sim_threshold, min_covariant_peers=a.min_peers,
        chunk_size=a.chunk_size, top_peers=a.top_peers, max_edges=a.max_edges,
        output_dir=os.path.join(a.out_dir, "shards"),
        checkpoint_secs=a.checkpoint_secs,
        max_rows=(a.max_rows or None),
        row_start_override=(a.row_start if a.row_start >= 0 else None),
        row_end_override=(a.row_end if a.row_end >= 0 else None),
    )

This is where the 64-dimensional dense descriptor is built and where the hub rule lives: an inbound cell is a hub if its peer count at |r| ≥ 0.85 puts its anomaly z-score in the top 5,000 of the corpus.

phase2/phase2_dense_anomaly_bank.py - the six descriptor families (320 lines, 14.2 KB)

from scipy.signal import hilbert

try:
    import torch
    import torch.nn as nn
    import torch.nn.functional as F
    TORCH_OK = True
except Exception:
    torch = None
    nn = None
    F = None
    TORCH_OK = False


FEATURE_NAMES = [
    "s_y", "int_t_dy", "int_y_dt", "levy_area", "quad_var", "cubic_var", "int_y2_dt", "int_t2_dy", "lead_lag_cov",
    "rqa_rr", "rqa_det", "rqa_lam", "rqa_diag_entropy",
    "ord_p0", "ord_p1", "ord_p2", "ord_p3", "ord_p4", "ord_p5", "perm_entropy", "forbidden_divergence",
    "arc_length", "elastic_energy", "mean_signed_curvature", "max_abs_curvature", "inflection_rate", "total_turning",

    "hilbert_freq_mean", "hilbert_freq_std", "hilbert_amp_kurt",
    "hvg_edge_density", "hvg_degree_mean", "hvg_degree_std", "hvg_degree_max",
    "cumulant4", "trispectrum_mag", "trispectrum_phase",
    "emd_extrema_rate", "emd_envelope_asymmetry", "emd_zero_cross_rate",
    "dist_skew", "dist_kurtosis", "range_ratio",
]

N_FEATURES = len(FEATURE_NAMES)
assert N_FEATURES == 64, N_FEATURES


def _safe_div(a, b, eps=1e-9):
    return a / (b + eps)


def _entropy_from_counts(counts, axis=-1):
    total = np.sum(counts, axis=axis, keepdims=True)
    p = counts / np.maximum(total, 1e-9)
    return -np.sum(p * np.log(p + 1e-9), axis=axis, keepdims=True)


def _hvg_features(y):
    n, length = y.shape
    degree = np.zeros((n, length), dtype=np.float64)
    total_edges = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    possible = 0
    max_lag = min(8, length - 1)
    for d in range(1, max_lag + 1):
        width = length - d + 1
        if d == 1:
            edge = np.ones((n, width), dtype=bool)
        else:
            mids = [y[:, i:i + width] for i in range(1, d)]
            middle_max = np.maximum.reduce(mids)
            edge = middle_max < np.minimum(y[:, :width], y[:, d - 1:])
        total_edges += edge.sum(axis=1)
        degree[:, :width] += edge
        degree[:, d - 1:] += edge
        possible += width
    possible = max(possible, 1)
    return np.column_stack([
        total_edges / possible,
        degree.mean(axis=1),
        degree.std(axis=1),
        degree.max(axis=1),
    ])


def _rqa_features(y):
    n, length = y.shape
    v0 = y[:, :-4]
    v1 = y[:, 2:-2]
    v2 = y[:, 4:]
    if v0.shape[1] < 4:
        rr = np.zeros((n, 1))
        det = np.zeros((n, 1))
        lam = np.zeros((n, 1))
        diag_entropy = np.zeros((n, 1))
        h1 = np.zeros((n, 1))
        return np.hstack([rr, det, lam, diag_entropy]), h1
    idx = np.linspace(0, v0.shape[1] - 1, min(16, v0.shape[1])).astype(int)
    v = np.stack([v0[:, idx], v1[:, idx], v2[:, idx]], axis=-1)
    dist_sq = np.sum((v[:, :, None, :] - v[:, None, :, :]) ** 2, axis=-1)
    eps = np.percentile(dist_sq, 25, axis=(1, 2), keepdims=True) + 1e-7
    rec = (dist_sq <= eps).astype(np.float64)
    eye = 1.0 - np.eye(rec.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)[None, :, :]
    rec_off = rec * eye
    total = rec_off.sum(axis=(1, 2), keepdims=True)
    rr = rec_off.mean(axis=(1, 2), keepdims=True)
    det_mat = rec_off[:, :-1, :-1] * rec_off[:, 1:, 1:]
    det = det_mat.sum(axis=(1, 2), keepdims=True)
    det = _safe_div(det, total)
    lam_mat = rec_off[:, :-1, :] * rec_off[:, 1:, :]
    lam = lam_mat.sum(axis=(1, 2), keepdims=True)
    lam = _safe_div(lam, total)
    diag_counts = []
    for lag in range(1, rec_off.shape[1]):
        c = np.sum(rec_off[:, :-lag, :-lag] * rec_off[:, lag:, lag:], axis=(1, 2))
        diag_counts.append(c[:, None])
    if diag_counts:
        diag_counts_arr = np.hstack(diag_counts)
    else:
        diag_counts_arr = np.zeros((n, 1))
    diag_entropy = _entropy_from_counts(diag_counts_arr, axis=1)
    h1 = np.min(dist_sq + np.eye(dist_sq.shape[1])[None, :, :] * 1e12, axis=(1, 2)).reshape(n, 1)
    return np.hstack([rr.reshape(n, 1), det.reshape(n, 1), lam.reshape(n, 1), diag_entropy.reshape(n, 1)]), h1


def _ordinal_features(y):
    w0 = y[:, :-2]
    w1 = y[:, 1:-1]
    w2 = y[:, 2:]
    c01 = (w0 < w1).astype(np.int32)
    c12 = (w1 < w2).astype(np.int32)
    c02 = (w0 < w2).astype(np.int32)
    code = c01 * 4 + c12 * 2 + c02
    valid = [0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 7]
    probs = np.stack([(code == c).mean(axis=1) for c in valid], axis=1)
    perm_entropy = -np.sum(probs * np.log(probs + 1e-9), axis=1, keepdims=True) / np.log(6.0)
    forbidden = np.sum((probs - (1.0 / 6.0)) ** 2, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    return np.hstack([probs, perm_entropy, forbidden])


def _scattering_features(y):
    parts = []
    for scale in (1, 2, 4, 8):
        if y.shape[1] <= scale:
            parts.extend([np.zeros((y.shape[0], 1))] * 3)
            continue
        d1 = np.abs(y[:, scale:] - y[:, :-scale])
        parts.append(d1.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True))
        parts.append(d1.std(axis=1, keepdims=True))
        if d1.shape[1] > 2:
            d2 = np.abs(d1[:, 2:] - d1[:, :-2])
            parts.append(d2.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True))
        else:
            parts.append(np.zeros((y.shape[0], 1)))
    return np.hstack(parts)


def compute_quartile_brute_force_features(y_zone):
    y = np.asarray(y_zone, dtype=np.float64)
    n, length = y.shape
    if length < 9:
        raise ValueError(f"zone too short: {length}")
    dy = np.diff(y, axis=1)
    ddy = np.diff(dy, axis=1)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, length, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    dt = 1.0 / (length - 1)
    y0 = y - y[:, 0:1]

    s_y = y0[:, -1:]
    int_t_dy = np.sum(t[:, :-1] * dy, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    int_y_dt = np.sum(y0[:, :-1] * dt, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    levy_area = 0.5 * (int_t_dy - int_y_dt)
    quad_var = np.sum(dy ** 2, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    cubic_var = np.sum(dy ** 3, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    int_y2_dt = np.sum((y0[:, :-1] ** 2) * dt, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    int_t2_dy = np.sum((t[:, :-1] ** 2) * dy, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    lead_lag_cov = np.sum(dy[:, 1:] * dy[:, :-1], axis=1, keepdims=True)
    rough = np.hstack([s_y, int_t_dy, int_y_dt, levy_area, quad_var, cubic_var, int_y2_dt, int_t2_dy, lead_lag_cov])

    rqa, h1_loop = _rqa_features(y)
    ordinal = _ordinal_features(y)

    ds = np.sqrt(dt ** 2 + dy ** 2)
    arc_length = np.sum(ds, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    kappa = (dt * ddy) / ((dt ** 2 + dy[:, :-1] ** 2) ** 1.5 + 1e-9)
    elastic_energy = np.mean(kappa ** 2, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    mean_signed_curvature = np.mean(kappa, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    max_abs_curvature = np.max(np.abs(kappa), axis=1, keepdims=True)
    inflection_rate = np.mean((kappa[:, 1:] * kappa[:, :-1]) < 0, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    angles = np.arctan2(dy, dt)
    total_turning = np.sum(np.abs(np.diff(angles, axis=1)), axis=1, keepdims=True)
    frenet = np.hstack([arc_length, elastic_energy, mean_signed_curvature, max_abs_curvature, inflection_rate, total_turning])

    scattering = _scattering_features(y)

    abs_dy = np.sort(np.abs(dy), axis=1)
    h0_max = abs_dy[:, -1:]
    h0_second = abs_dy[:, -2:-1]
    h0_entropy = _entropy_from_counts(abs_dy, axis=1)
    hvg = _hvg_features(y)
    topology = np.hstack([h0_max.reshape(n, 1), h0_second.reshape(n, 1), h0_entropy.reshape(n, 1), h1_loop.reshape(n, 1)])

    tau1 = np.mean(np.abs(y[:, 1:] - y[:, :-1]), axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7
    tau4 = np.mean(np.abs(y[:, 4:] - y[:, :-4]), axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7
    hurst_q1 = np.log2(tau4 / tau1) / 2.0
    tau1_q2 = np.sqrt(np.mean((y[:, 1:] - y[:, :-1]) ** 2, axis=1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-7
    tau4_q2 = np.sqrt(np.mean((y[:, 4:] - y[:, :-4]) ** 2, axis=1, keepdims=True)) + 1e-7
    hurst_q2 = np.log2(tau4_q2 / tau1_q2) / 2.0
    multifractal_width = hurst_q1 - hurst_q2
    max_planar_dist = np.max(np.abs(y - y[:, 0:1]), axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7
    katz_dim = np.log10(length) / (np.log10(max_planar_dist / (arc_length + 1e-7)) + np.log10(length) + 1e-7)
    higuchi_proxy = np.log(arc_length + 1e-7) / (np.log(max_planar_dist + 1e-7) + 1e-7)
    multifractal = np.hstack([hurst_q1, hurst_q2, multifractal_width, katz_dim, higuchi_proxy])

    analytic = hilbert(y0, axis=1)
    inst_amp = np.abs(analytic)
    inst_phase = np.unwrap(np.angle(analytic), axis=1)
    inst_freq = np.diff(inst_phase, axis=1)
    hfreq_mean = np.mean(inst_freq, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    hfreq_std = np.std(inst_freq, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    amp_kurt = np.mean(((inst_amp - inst_amp.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / (inst_amp.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7)) ** 4, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    hilbert_feats = np.hstack([hfreq_mean, hfreq_std, amp_kurt])

    spectrum = np.fft.rfft(y, axis=1) / length
    tris = []
    for k1, k2 in ((1, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3)):
        if k1 + k2 < spectrum.shape[1]:
            val = spectrum[:, k1] * spectrum[:, k2] * np.conj(spectrum[:, k1 + k2])
            tris.append(np.abs(val)[:, None])
            tris.append(np.angle(val)[:, None])
    if tris:
        tris_arr = np.hstack(tris)
        tris_mag = np.mean(tris_arr[:, 0::2], axis=1, keepdims=True)
        tris_phase = np.mean(tris_arr[:, 1::2], axis=1, keepdims=True)
    else:
        tris_mag = np.zeros((n, 1))
        tris_phase = np.zeros((n, 1))
    centered = y - y.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    std = y.std(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7
    z = centered / std
    cumulant4 = np.mean(z ** 4, axis=1, keepdims=True) - 3.0
    trispectrum = np.hstack([cumulant4.reshape(n, 1), tris_mag.reshape(n, 1), tris_phase.reshape(n, 1)])

    diff_sign = np.diff(np.signbit(y).astype(np.int8), axis=1)
