    extremas = np.abs(np.diff(np.signbit(dy).astype(np.int8), axis=1)).sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    emd_extrema_rate = extremas / max(length - 2, 1)
    med = np.median(y, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    emd_env_asym = (y.max(axis=1, keepdims=True) - med) / (med - y.min(axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-7)
    emd_zero_cross = np.mean(diff_sign != 0, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    emd = np.hstack([emd_extrema_rate.reshape(n, 1), emd_env_asym.reshape(n, 1), emd_zero_cross.reshape(n, 1)])

    dist_skew = np.mean(z ** 3, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    dist_kurt = np.mean(z ** 4, axis=1, keepdims=True)
    range_ratio = (y.max(axis=1, keepdims=True) - y.min(axis=1, keepdims=True)) / std
    higher = np.hstack([dist_skew.reshape(n, 1), dist_kurt.reshape(n, 1), range_ratio.reshape(n, 1)])

    raw = np.hstack([
        rough, rqa, ordinal, frenet, scattering, topology, multifractal,
        hilbert_feats, hvg, trispectrum, emd, higher,
    ]).astype(np.float32)
    return np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


if TORCH_OK:
    class LearnableGeometricAnomalyBank(nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, seq_len=38, num_wavelets=16, rqa_kernels=8, out_dim=128):
            super().__init__()
            self.num_wavelets = num_wavelets
            self.wavelet_freqs = nn.Parameter(torch.linspace(1.0, 18.0, num_wavelets))
            self.wavelet_scales = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(num_wavelets) * 0.15)
            self.wavelet_phases = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(num_wavelets))
            self.delay_conv = nn.Conv1d(1, rqa_kernels, kernel_size=5, stride=1, dilation=2, padding=4)
            self.rqa_log_eps = nn.Parameter(torch.linspace(-2.0, 1.0, rqa_kernels))
            self.rqa_temp = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(rqa_kernels) * 5.0)
            self.lead_lag_bilinear = nn.Bilinear(8, 8, 32)
            self.path_proj = nn.Linear(1, 8)
            total = num_wavelets * 2 + rqa_kernels * 2 + 32 + 4
            self.fusion_net = nn.Sequential(
                nn.Linear(total, 256),
                nn.GELU(),
                nn.LayerNorm(256),
                nn.Linear(256, out_dim),
                nn.GELU(),
                nn.LayerNorm(out_dim),
            )

        def forward(self, y_ticks):
            b, length = y_ticks.shape
            t = torch.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, length, device=y_ticks.device)[None, None, :]
            freqs = self.wavelet_freqs[None, :, None]
            scales = torch.clamp(self.wavelet_scales[None, :, None].abs(), min=0.03)
            phases = self.wavelet_phases[None, :, None]
            envelope = torch.exp(-0.5 * (t / scales) ** 2)
            kernel_real = envelope * torch.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freqs * t + phases)
            kernel_imag = envelope * torch.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freqs * t + phases)
            y_exp = y_ticks[:, None, :]
            w_real = torch.mean(y_exp * kernel_real, dim=-1)
            w_imag = torch.mean(y_exp * kernel_imag, dim=-1)
            w_modulus = torch.sqrt(w_real ** 2 + w_imag ** 2 + 1e-8)
            w_phase = torch.atan2(w_imag, w_real + 1e-8)

            h_delay = self.delay_conv(y_exp)
            idx = torch.linspace(0, length - 1, min(12, length), device=y_ticks.device).long()
            h_sub = h_delay[:, :, idx]
            dist = (h_sub[:, :, :, None] - h_sub[:, :, None, :]) ** 2
            eps = torch.exp(self.rqa_log_eps)[None, :, None, None]
            temp = torch.clamp(self.rqa_temp.abs(), min=1.0)[None, :, None, None]
            soft_rec = torch.sigmoid(temp * (eps - dist))
            rqa_density = torch.mean(soft_rec, dim=(-1, -2))
            rqa_diag = torch.mean(soft_rec[:, :, :-1, :-1] * soft_rec[:, :, 1:, 1:], dim=(-1, -2))

            dy = y_ticks[:, 1:] - y_ticks[:, :-1]
            h_path = self.path_proj(dy[:, :, None])
            cum_path = torch.cumsum(h_path, dim=1) / length
            lead_vec = torch.mean(h_path[:, 1:, :], dim=1)
            lag_vec = torch.mean(cum_path[:, :-1, :], dim=1)
            rough_sig = self.lead_lag_bilinear(lead_vec, lag_vec)

            ddy = dy[:, 1:] - dy[:, :-1]
            dt = 1.0 / length
            kappa = (dt * ddy) / ((dt ** 2 + dy[:, :-1] ** 2) ** 1.5 + 1e-8)
            curv_energy = torch.mean(kappa ** 2, dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            curv_skew = torch.mean(kappa ** 3, dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            levy = 0.5 * (
                torch.sum(t[:, 0, :-1] * dy, dim=-1, keepdim=True)
                - torch.sum(y_ticks[:, :-1] * dt, dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            )
            drift = y_ticks[:, -1:] - y_ticks[:, 0:1]
            combined = torch.cat([
                w_modulus, w_phase, rqa_density, rqa_diag,
                rough_sig, curv_energy, curv_skew, levy, drift,
            ], dim=-1)
            return self.fusion_net(combined)

Read this carefully in conjunction with Chapter 15. The descriptor is a good description of a path and a poor discriminator between paths: the six families overlap in what they measure, which is the root of the collinearity documented in Chapter 6.

phase2/phase2_step2_inbound_outbound_ai.py - outbound evaluation and vault construction (321 lines, 14.2 KB)
"""Phase 2 Step 2 - CZET inbound foundation training + outbound vault verification.

Adapted to the whitened feature bank (v2):
  Q_seq(cell, quartile, 72) = [desc_Qk (17) | dense_qk whitened, padded to 55]
  trigger_vec_dense (N,132) / resolution_vec_dense (N,138) for the similarity engine

Modes:
  --mode train   train pass 1 (bidirectional masked quartile reconstruction) + pass 2
                 (covariant-pair metric learning) -> czet_phase2.pt
  --mode vault   score outbound triggers against the trained hubs -> strategy vault
  --mode both    (default)

Outputs (in --phase2_dir):
  czet_phase2.pt, czet_train_log.json, phase2_strategy_vault.parquet,
  phase2_anomaly_strategies.xlsx (if openpyxl), phase2_step2_report.json
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import argparse
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))

try:
    import pqcompat
except Exception:
    pqcompat = None

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


def read_table(path):
    if path.endswith(".parquet"):
        try:
            if pqcompat is not None:
                return pqcompat.read_parquet(path)
            return pd.read_parquet(path)
        except Exception:
            alt = path[:-len(".parquet")] + ".csv"
            if os.path.exists(alt):
                return pd.read_csv(alt)
            raise
    return pd.read_csv(path)


class CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, zone_dim=72, d_model=256, nhead=4, num_layers=4, dim_ff=768):
        super().__init__()
        self.zone_proj = nn.Linear(zone_dim, d_model)
        self.pos_embed = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(1, 4, d_model) * 0.02)
        layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(
            d_model=d_model, nhead=nhead, dim_feedforward=dim_ff,
            dropout=0.1, activation="gelu", batch_first=True, norm_first=True,
        )
        self.transformer = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, num_layers=num_layers)
        self.zone_decoder = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.LayerNorm(d_model),
            nn.Linear(d_model, zone_dim),
        )
        self.covariant_proj = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model * 2, d_model), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(d_model, 128),
        )
        self.anomaly_head = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(d_model * 2, 128), nn.GELU(), nn.LayerNorm(128), nn.Linear(128, 3),
        )

    def encode(self, q, mask=None):
        x = self.zone_proj(q) + self.pos_embed
        if mask is not None:
            x = x.clone()
            x[:, mask, :] = 0.0
        return self.transformer(x)

    def forward(self, q):
        h_fwd = self.encode(q, [2, 3])
        pred_q34 = self.zone_decoder(h_fwd[:, 2:4, :])
        h_bwd = self.encode(q, [0, 1])
        pred_q12 = self.zone_decoder(h_bwd[:, 0:2, :])
        h_early = torch.cat([h_fwd[:, 0, :], h_fwd[:, 1, :]], dim=-1)
        z_cov = F.normalize(self.covariant_proj(h_early), dim=-1)
        anom = self.anomaly_head(h_early)
        return pred_q34, pred_q12, z_cov, anom


def train(phase2_dir, epochs1, epochs2, batch_size, d_model, layers, nhead, ff,
          max_pairs, seed=1337, max_train_rows=0, resume=False):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    cache = np.load(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_train_cache.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    Q = cache["Q_seq"]
    N = Q.shape[0]
    zone_dim = Q.shape[2]
    n_in = int(N * 0.85)
    if max_train_rows and max_train_rows < n_in:
        train_idx = np.sort(np.random.permutation(n_in)[:max_train_rows]).astype(np.int64)
    else:
        train_idx = np.arange(n_in, dtype=np.int64)
    n_tr = len(train_idx)
    print(f"[*] Q_seq={Q.shape} inbound={n_in:,} outbound={N - n_in:,} train_rows={n_tr:,} "
          f"| threads={torch.get_num_threads()}", flush=True)

    model = CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer(zone_dim=zone_dim, d_model=d_model, nhead=nhead,
                                         num_layers=layers, dim_ff=ff)
    if resume and os.path.exists(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_phase2.pt")):
        ck = torch.load(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_phase2.pt"), map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
        model.load_state_dict(ck["state_dict"])
        print("[*] resumed weights from czet_phase2.pt", ck.get("cfg"), flush=True)
    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
    print(f"[*] CZET params={n_params:,}", flush=True)
    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
    held = cache["protected_held"]
    ret = cache["q4_net_return"]
    log = {"params": int(n_params), "zone_dim": int(zone_dim), "inbound": n_in, "train_rows": int(n_tr),
           "batch_size": batch_size, "pass1": [], "pass2": []}

    steps = n_tr // batch_size
    model.train()
    for ep in range(1, epochs1 + 1):
        perm = train_idx[np.random.permutation(n_tr)]
        tot, t0 = 0.0, time.time()
        for s in range(steps):
            idx = np.sort(perm[s * batch_size:(s + 1) * batch_size])
            bq = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[idx]))
            b_held = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(held[idx].astype(np.float32)))
            b_ret = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(ret[idx]))
            opt.zero_grad()
            p34, p12, _, anom = model(bq)
            loss = (F.mse_loss(p34, bq[:, 2:4, :]) + F.mse_loss(p12, bq[:, 0:2, :])
                    + 0.25 * (F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(anom[:, 0], b_held)
                              + F.smooth_l1_loss(anom[:, 2], b_ret)))
            loss.backward()
            opt.step()
            tot += loss.item()
        msg = f"    pass1 epoch {ep}/{epochs1} | loss {tot / steps:.5f} | {time.time() - t0:.0f}s"
        print(msg, flush=True)
        log["pass1"].append({"epoch": ep, "loss": round(tot / steps, 5), "secs": round(time.time() - t0, 1)})
        torch.save({"state_dict": model.state_dict(),
                    "cfg": {"zone_dim": zone_dim, "d_model": d_model, "nhead": nhead,
                            "num_layers": layers, "dim_ff": ff}},
                   os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_phase2.pt"))

    edges = None
    for name in ("phase2_covariant_edges.parquet", "phase2_covariant_edges.csv"):
        p = os.path.join(phase2_dir, name)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            edges = read_table(p)
            break
    if edges is not None and len(edges):
        e = edges[(edges["src_idx"] < n_in) & (edges["dst_idx"] < n_in)]
        if len(e) > max_pairs > 0:
            e = e.sample(n=max_pairs, random_state=seed)
        print(f"[*] pass2 pairs={len(e):,}", flush=True)
        opt2 = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
        src = e["src_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        dst = e["dst_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        sign = np.sign(e["cov_0_50"].to_numpy(np.float32))
        lock = np.clip(e["lock_50_100"].to_numpy(np.float32), -1, 1)
        steps2 = len(e) // batch_size
        for ep in range(1, epochs2 + 1):
            perm = np.random.permutation(len(e))
            tot, t0 = 0.0, time.time()
            for s in range(steps2):
                j = np.sort(perm[s * batch_size:(s + 1) * batch_size])
                qz = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[src[j]]))
                qy = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[dst[j]]))
                b_sign = torch.from_numpy(sign[j])
                b_lock = torch.from_numpy(lock[j])
                opt2.zero_grad()
                p34z, _, zz, anomz = model(qz)
                _, _, zy, _ = model(qy)
                cos = F.cosine_similarity(zz, zy * b_sign[:, None], dim=-1)
                loss_cov = F.mse_loss(cos, b_lock)
                w = torch.sigmoid(b_lock * 3.0)[:, None, None]
                loss_lock = torch.mean(w * (p34z - qz[:, 2:4, :]) ** 2)
                loss_prob = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(anomz[:, 1], (b_lock >= 0.65).float())
                loss = loss_cov + 2.0 * loss_lock + 0.5 * loss_prob
                loss.backward()
                opt2.step()
                tot += loss.item()
            msg = f"    pass2 epoch {ep}/{epochs2} | loss {tot / max(1, steps2):.5f} | {time.time() - t0:.0f}s"
            print(msg, flush=True)
            log["pass2"].append({"epoch": ep, "loss": round(tot / max(1, steps2), 5),
                                 "secs": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "pairs": int(len(e))})
    torch.save({"state_dict": model.state_dict(),
                "cfg": {"zone_dim": zone_dim, "d_model": d_model, "nhead": nhead,
                        "num_layers": layers, "dim_ff": ff}},
               os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_phase2.pt"))
    with open(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "czet_train_log.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(log, f, indent=2)
    return model, log


def vault(phase2_dir, top_hubs=5000, thr=0.85, min_hits=10):
    scan = np.load(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "jump10_phase2_scan.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    T = scan["trigger_vec_dense"]
    R = scan["resolution_vec_dense"]
    N = T.shape[0]
    n_in = int(N * 0.85)

    db = None
    for name in ("phase2_covariant_db.parquet", "phase2_covariant_db.csv"):
        p = os.path.join(phase2_dir, name)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            db = read_table(p)
            break
    if db is None or not len(db):
        print("[-] no covariant db; skipping vault", flush=True)
        return 0

    db = db[db["anchor_cell_id"] <= n_in].sort_values("anomaly_z_score", ascending=False)
    hubs = db.head(top_hubs).reset_index(drop=True)
    hub_idx = (hubs["anchor_cell_id"].to_numpy(np.int64) - 1)
    hub_idx = hub_idx[(hub_idx >= 0) & (hub_idx < n_in)]
    Th = np.ascontiguousarray(T[hub_idx])
    Rh = np.ascontiguousarray(R[hub_idx])
    held = scan["protected_held"]
    ret = scan["q4_net_return"]
    print(f"[*] vault: {len(hub_idx):,} hubs x {N - n_in:,} outbound triggers", flush=True)

    rows = []
    CH = 20000
    for h0 in range(0, len(hub_idx), 256):
        h1 = min(len(hub_idx), h0 + 256)
        sim_hits = [[] for _ in range(h1 - h0)]
        for s in range(n_in, N, CH):
            e = min(N, s + CH)
            sims = T[s:e] @ Th[h0:h1].T
            big = np.abs(sims) >= thr
            if big.any():
                ri, ci = np.nonzero(big)
                for r, c in zip(ri, ci):
                    sim_hits[c].append((s + r, sims[r, c]))
        for ci in range(h1 - h0):
            if not sim_hits[ci]:
                continue
            idx = np.fromiter((x[0] for x in sim_hits[ci]), dtype=np.int64, count=len(sim_hits[ci]))
            if len(idx) < min_hits:
                continue
            pol = np.fromiter((np.sign(x[1]) for x in sim_hits[ci]), dtype=np.float32, count=len(idx))
            h = h0 + ci
            cont = float(np.mean((R[idx] * pol[:, None]) @ Rh[h]))
            prot = float(np.mean(held[idx]))
            dirret = ret[idx] * pol
            hrow = hubs.iloc[h]
            expected = -1.0 if str(hrow["recommended_action"]) == "SELL_PROTECTED_HIGH" else 1.0
            dirwin = float(np.mean(np.sign(dirret) == expected))
            rows.append({
                "anchor_cell_id": int(hrow["anchor_cell_id"]),
                "outbound_triggers": int(len(idx)),
                "outbound_50_100_lock_corr": round(cont, 4),
                "outbound_protected_win_rate": round(prot, 4),
                "outbound_directional_win_rate": round(dirwin, 4),
                "outbound_verified_edge": round(max(prot, dirwin), 4),
            })
        print(f"    hubs {h0}-{h1} done", flush=True)

    v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
    if not v.empty:
        v = v.merge(hubs, on="anchor_cell_id", how="left", suffixes=("", "_db"))
        v = v[v["outbound_verified_edge"] >= 0.55].copy()
        nn = v["outbound_triggers"].to_numpy(float)
        pp = v["outbound_verified_edge"].clip(0.0, 1.0).to_numpy(float)
        zc = 1.959964
        lb = (pp + zc ** 2 / (2 * nn) - zc * np.sqrt(pp * (1 - pp) / nn + zc ** 2 / (4 * nn ** 2))) / (1 + zc ** 2 / nn)
        v["outbound_win_lb95"] = np.round(lb, 4)
        v["sample_flag"] = np.where(nn >= 30, "OK", "THIN")
        v = v.sort_values(["outbound_win_lb95", "outbound_triggers"], ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
        v.insert(0, "strategy_id", [f"STRAT_J10_{i + 1:04d}" for i in range(len(v))])
        v["phase3_status"] = "READY_FOR_EQUITY_SIM"
    out_p = os.path.join(phase2_dir, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet")
    if pqcompat is not None and not v.empty:
        pqcompat.write_parquet(v, out_p)
    else:
        out_p = out_p.replace(".parquet", ".csv")
        v.to_csv(out_p, index=False)
    print(f"[+] vault: {len(v):,} verified strategies -> {out_p}", flush=True)
    try:
        with pd.ExcelWriter(os.path.join(phase2_dir, "phase2_anomaly_strategies.xlsx"),
                            engine="openpyxl") as w:
            v.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Phase3_Ready_Strategies", index=False)
            db.head(10000).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Inbound_Covariant_Hubs", index=False)
        print("[+] xlsx written", flush=True)
    except Exception as ex:
        print(f"[-] xlsx skipped: {type(ex).__name__} {ex}", flush=True)
    return len(v)


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--phase2_dir", default=r"C:\cluster\phase2")
    ap.add_argument("--mode", default="both", choices=["train", "vault", "both"])
    ap.add_argument("--epochs_pass1", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--epochs_pass2", type=int, default=3)
    ap.add_argument("--batch_size", type=int, default=512)
    ap.add_argument("--d_model", type=int, default=256)
    ap.add_argument("--layers", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--nhead", type=int, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--dim_ff", type=int, default=768)
    ap.add_argument("--max_pairs", type=int, default=300000)
    ap.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--top_hubs", type=int, default=5000)
    ap.add_argument("--max_train_rows", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--resume", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    if a.threads > 0:
        torch.set_num_threads(a.threads)
    if a.mode in ("train", "both"):
        train(a.phase2_dir, a.epochs_pass1, a.epochs_pass2, a.batch_size,
              a.d_model, a.layers, a.nhead, a.dim_ff, a.max_pairs,
              max_train_rows=a.max_train_rows, resume=a.resume)
    if a.mode in ("vault", "both"):
        vault(a.phase2_dir, a.top_hubs)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Line for line, this is the most consequential file in the project: the verified_edge = max(protected, directional) assignment appears here, together with the sweet-spot filter and the 10-trigger minimum.

phase2/fix_collapse_and_whiten.py - covariance whitening that produced the 132-d space (283 lines, 11.4 KB)
"""
PHASE 2 - COLLINEAR-COLLAPSE REPAIR
Implements C:\\cluster\\phase2_mathematicalfix.md:

  Bug A: single-shot bicoherence identity  |A*B*conj(C)| / (|A*B|*|C|) == 1.0 exactly
  Bug B: common-mode positive cone  ->  every cell at |r| >= 0.85 with every anchor

Fixes:
  * cross-sectional standardization (per feature)
  * SVD/PCA strip of the common-mode component(s) + Mahalanobis whitening
  * L2 unit-sphere projection    =>  E[r] = 0.0, sigma_r ~ 1/sqrt(D)
  * bicoherence replaced by phase-triad closure terms cos(phi1+phi2-phi3)

Memory-safe: mmap reads, two-pass chunked standardization, disk-backed outputs,
streamed zip writing. Never holds more than one float32 block of the matrix.
"""
import os
import sys
import io
import json
import time
import zipfile
import numpy as np

RAW = r"C:\cluster\phase2\jump10_phase2_subzones_raw.npz"
OUT = r"C:\cluster\phase2\jump10_phase2_subzones.npz"
TMP = r"C:\cluster\phase2\_white_tmp"
WNPZ = r"C:\cluster\phase2\whiten_transforms.npz"
REPORT = r"C:\cluster\phase2\whiten_report.json"

WHITEN_KEYS = [
    "trigger_vec", "resolution_vec",
    "trigger_vec_dense", "resolution_vec_dense",
    "dense_q1", "dense_q2", "dense_q3", "dense_q4", "dense_h1", "dense_h2",
]
CHUNK = 65536
SUBSAMPLE = 50000
STRIP_TOP_PC = 1
EPS = 1e-5
SEED = 42


def log(msg):
    print(msg, flush=True)


def two_pass_stats(A, chunk=CHUNK):
    n, d = A.shape
    s1 = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    s2 = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(0, n, chunk):
        j = min(i + chunk, n)
        b = np.asarray(A[i:j], dtype=np.float64)
        s1 += b.sum(axis=0)
        s2 += (b * b).sum(axis=0)
    mu = s1 / n
    var = np.maximum(s2 / n - mu * mu, 0.0)
    return mu.astype(np.float32), np.sqrt(var).astype(np.float32)


def fit_whitener(A, valid_cols, mu, sd):
    """Covariance on a random subsample of standardized data -> whitening W."""
    n = A.shape[0]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    idx = np.sort(rng.choice(n, size=min(SUBSAMPLE, n), replace=False))
    mu_sub = np.asarray(mu, dtype=np.float32)[valid_cols]
    sd_sub = np.asarray(sd, dtype=np.float32)[valid_cols]
    sub = np.empty((len(idx), int(valid_cols.sum())), dtype=np.float32)
    pos = 0
    for k in range(0, len(idx), CHUNK):
        sl = idx[k:k + CHUNK]
        block = np.asarray(A[sl], dtype=np.float32)[:, valid_cols]
        block = np.clip((block - mu_sub) / sd_sub, -6.0, 6.0)
        sub[pos:pos + block.shape[0]] = block
        pos += block.shape[0]

    cov = (sub.T @ sub) / sub.shape[0]
    cov = 0.5 * (cov + cov.T)
    U, S, _ = np.linalg.svd(cov, full_matrices=False)
    var_share = (S / max(S.sum(), 1e-12)) * 100.0
    start_pc = STRIP_TOP_PC if (S[0] / S.sum() > 0.35) else 0
    keep = np.arange(len(S)) >= start_pc
    keep &= S > 1e-3
    U_keep, S_keep = U[:, keep], S[keep]
    W = (U_keep @ np.diag(1.0 / np.sqrt(S_keep + EPS))).astype(np.float32)
    info = {
        "n_features_in": int(A.shape[1]),
        "n_constant_cols_dropped": int((~valid_cols).sum()),
        "n_features_kept": int(valid_cols.sum()),
        "n_components_out": int(W.shape[1]),
        "top5_eigenvalue_variance_share_pct": [round(float(x), 3) for x in var_share[:5]],
        "stripped_top_pc": int(start_pc),
    }
    return W, info


def transform_chunked(A, valid_cols, mu, sd, W, out_path, chunk=CHUNK):
    """y = L2normalize( clip((x-mu)/sd, +-6) @ W ) written to a disk-backed .npy"""
    n = A.shape[0]
    d_out = W.shape[1]
    mu_sub = np.asarray(mu, dtype=np.float32)[valid_cols]
    sd_sub = np.asarray(sd, dtype=np.float32)[valid_cols]
    out = np.lib.format.open_memmap(out_path, mode="w+", dtype=np.float32, shape=(n, d_out))
    for i in range(0, n, chunk):
        j = min(i + chunk, n)
        b = np.asarray(A[i:j], dtype=np.float32)[:, valid_cols]
        b = np.clip((b - mu_sub) / sd_sub, -6.0, 6.0)
        y = b @ W
        nrm = np.linalg.norm(y, axis=1, keepdims=True)
        nrm[nrm < 1e-9] = 1.0
        out[i:j] = y / nrm
    out.flush()
    del out
    return out_path


def main():
    t0 = time.time()
    if not os.path.exists(RAW):
        log(f"[-] raw npz not found: {RAW}")
        sys.exit(1)
    os.makedirs(TMP, exist_ok=True)

    log(f"[*] Opening {RAW} (mmap, no full load)")
    src = np.load(RAW, mmap_mode="r")
    keys = list(src.files)
    log(f"[*] {len(keys)} arrays: " + ", ".join(keys))
    report = {"input": RAW, "output": OUT, "arrays": {}, "whiteners": {}}

    # ---------------- pass 1: fix bicoherence BEFORE touching desc_Q1 --------
    log("\n[*] Replacing bicoherence with true phase-triad closure ...")
    q1 = np.asarray(src["desc_Q1"], dtype=np.float32)
    phi1 = np.arctan2(q1[:, 8], q1[:, 0] + 1e-9)
    phi2 = np.arctan2(q1[:, 9], q1[:, 1] + 1e-9)
    phi3 = np.arctan2(q1[:, 10], q1[:, 2] + 1e-9)
    triad = phi1 + phi2 - phi3
    bico_fixed = (0.5 * (np.abs(np.cos(triad)) + np.abs(np.sin(triad)))).astype(np.float32)
    phase_closure_cos = np.abs(np.cos(triad)).astype(np.float32)
    bico_old = np.asarray(src["bicoherence"], dtype=np.float32)
    report["bicoherence"] = {
        "old_min": float(bico_old.min()), "old_median": float(np.median(bico_old)),
        "old_max": float(bico_old.max()), "old_std": float(bico_old.std()),
        "new_min": float(bico_fixed.min()), "new_median": float(np.median(bico_fixed)),
        "new_max": float(bico_fixed.max()), "new_std": float(bico_fixed.std()),
        "phase_closure_abs_cos_median": float(np.median(phase_closure_cos)),
    }
    log(f"    old bico: min={bico_old.min():.6f} med={np.median(bico_old):.6f} "
        f"max={bico_old.max():.6f} (std={bico_old.std():.2e})")
    log(f"    new bico: min={bico_fixed.min():.6f} med={np.median(bico_fixed):.6f} "
        f"max={bico_fixed.max():.6f} (std={bico_fixed.std():.6f})")
    del bico_old, triad

    # ---------------- pass 2: whiten each descriptor matrix ------------------
    whitened_paths = {}
    for key in WHITEN_KEYS:
        if key not in src.files:
            log(f"[!] skip missing {key}")
            continue
        t1 = time.time()
        A = src[key]
        n, d = A.shape
        log(f"\n[*] Whitening `{key}` shape={A.shape} ...")
        mu, sd = two_pass_stats(A)
        sd_safe = np.where(sd < 1e-6, 1.0, sd)
        valid = sd >= 1e-6
        if valid.sum() < 2:
            log(f"[!] {key}: <2 non-constant columns, passing through")
            continue
        W, info = fit_whitener(A, valid, mu, sd_safe)
        p = os.path.join(TMP, f"{key}.npy")
        transform_chunked(A, valid, mu, sd_safe, W, p)
        whitened_paths[key] = p
        info["seconds"] = round(time.time() - t1, 2)
        report["whiteners"][key] = info
        report["arrays"][key] = {"in_shape": [n, d], "out_shape": [n, info["n_components_out"]]}
        log(f"    -> kept {info['n_features_kept']}/{d} cols, {info['n_components_out']} comps, "
            f"strip_top_pc={info['stripped_top_pc']}, top5 var%={info['top5_eigenvalue_variance_share_pct']}, "
            f"{info['seconds']}s")
        # persist W for later live/outbound scoring
        np.save(os.path.join(TMP, f"W_{key}.npy"), W)
        np.save(os.path.join(TMP, f"mu_{key}.npy"), mu)
        np.save(os.path.join(TMP, f"sd_{key}.npy"), sd_safe)
        np.save(os.path.join(TMP, f"cols_{key}.npy"), valid)
        del A, mu, sd, W

    # ---------------- pass 3: stream-write the repaired npz -----------------
    log(f"\n[*] Writing {OUT} ...")
    big_out = {k: np.load(p, mmap_mode="r") for k, p in whitened_paths.items()}
    tmp_out = OUT + ".tmp"
    order = []
    for k in keys:
        if k in whitened_paths:
            order.append(k)
    order += [k for k in keys if k not in whitened_paths]
    with zipfile.ZipFile(tmp_out, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as zf:
        for k in order:
            if k in big_out:
                with zf.open(f"{k}.npy", "w", force_zip64=True) as fh:
                    np.save(fh, big_out[k])
                continue
            if k == "bicoherence":
                arr = bico_fixed
            else:
                arr = np.asarray(src[k])
            with zf.open(f"{k}.npy", "w", force_zip64=True) as fh:
                np.save(fh, arr)
            if k == "bicoherence":
                with zf.open("phase_closure_abs_cos.npy", "w", force_zip64=True) as fh:
                    np.save(fh, phase_closure_cos)
    os.replace(tmp_out, OUT)
    size = os.path.getsize(OUT)
    log(f"[+] wrote {OUT} ({size/1e9:.2f} GB)")
    report["output_bytes"] = size
    report["elapsed_seconds"] = round(time.time() - t0, 1)

    # ---------------- pass 4: verification ---------------------------------
    log("\n--- VERIFYING DE-COLLAPSED COVARIANT SCORES ACROSS 591,171 CELLS ---")
    new = np.load(OUT, mmap_mode="r")
    T = new["trigger_vec_dense"] if "trigger_vec_dense" in new.files else new["trigger_vec"]
    R = new["resolution_vec_dense"] if "resolution_vec_dense" in new.files else new["resolution_vec"]
    q4_ret = new["q4_net_return"]
    held = new["protected_held"]
    d = T.shape[1]
    log(f"[*] T shape={T.shape}  (expected sigma_r ~ {1/np.sqrt(d):.4f})")

    anchors = [100, 500, 1000, 5000, 50000, 250000]
    verify = []
    for a in anchors:
        t1 = time.time()
        sims = T @ T[a]
        sims[max(0, a - 2): min(T.shape[0], a + 3)] = 0.0
        abs_sims = np.abs(sims)
        top5 = np.sort(abs_sims)[::-1][:5]
        rec = {
            "anchor": a,
            "top5_abs_r": [round(float(x), 4) for x in top5],
            "peers_ge_070": int((abs_sims >= 0.70).sum()),
            "peers_ge_080": int((abs_sims >= 0.80).sum()),
            "peers_ge_085": int((abs_sims >= 0.85).sum()),
            "mean_r": round(float(sims.mean()), 6),
            "p999_abs_r": round(float(np.percentile(abs_sims, 99.9)), 4),
            "seconds": round(time.time() - t1, 2),
        }
        verify.append(rec)
        log(f"Anchor {a:6d}: top5 |r|={rec['top5_abs_r']} | peers>=0.70: {rec['peers_ge_070']:5d} "
            f"| >=0.80: {rec['peers_ge_080']:4d} | >=0.85: {rec['peers_ge_085']:3d} "
            f"| mean r={rec['mean_r']:+.5f} | p99.9={rec['p999_abs_r']:.3f}")
    report["anchor_verification"] = verify

    # population scan: how many rows would pass min_peers / pathology check
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    sample = np.sort(rng.choice(T.shape[0], size=300, replace=False))
    counts85, counts70, means = [], [], []
    for a in sample:
        s = T @ T[int(a)]
        s[max(0, int(a) - 2): min(T.shape[0], int(a) + 3)] = 0.0
        aa = np.abs(s)
        counts85.append(int((aa >= 0.85).sum()))
        counts70.append(int((aa >= 0.70).sum()))
        means.append(float(s.mean()))
    counts85 = np.array(counts85)
    counts70 = np.array(counts70)
    report["population_sample"] = {
        "rows_sampled": int(len(sample)),
        "mean_r_of_means": round(float(np.mean(means)), 6),
        "median_peers_ge_085": int(np.median(counts85)),
        "p90_peers_ge_085": int(np.percentile(counts85, 90)),
        "p99_peers_ge_085": int(np.percentile(counts85, 99)),
        "median_peers_ge_070": int(np.median(counts70)),
        "frac_rows_with_ge12_peers_085": round(float((counts85 >= 12).mean()), 4),
        "frac_rows_with_ge12_peers_070": round(float((counts70 >= 12).mean()), 4),
    }
    log("\n[*] Population sample of 300 random anchors:")
    for k, v in report["population_sample"].items():
        log(f"      {k}: {v}")
    log(f"\n[+] total elapsed {report['elapsed_seconds']}s")
    with open(REPORT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
    log(f"[+] report -> {REPORT}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

The whitening transform built here was applied in memory and never written to disk. That single omission is why the live tester cannot reproduce the backtest's matches (Chapter 16).

phase2/phase2_czet_sweetspot.py - CZET v2 architecture, masked reconstruction, PLS blend (333 lines, 14.9 KB)
"""Item 06 - CZET v2 on the covariant sweet-spot manifold, LearnableGeometricAnomalyBank attached.

Universe   : hubs with 12 <= peers <= 240 and 0.85 <= mean|r| <= 0.955, plus every cell that
             is an endpoint of an edge inside that set (all cells < n_in).
Predictors : self q1/q2 (144) + peer-family mean of q1/q2 (144) + block macro descriptor (64).
Pass 1     : masked quartile reconstruction (q3/q4 from q1/q2) + protected/return heads.
Pass 2     : covariant metric learning. score = cosine + pair head(zz, zy, zz*zy, |zz-zy|,
             cos, observed cov). loss = value + BCE + weighted q3/q4 alignment + rank loss.
Linear path: 24-component PLS on the 352-d predictor block, fitted on train rows only,
             stacked with the ensembled net output by a train-fitted scalar blend.
Evaluation : R2 on held-out rows, Spearman / precision@10% on held-out edges
             (edges touching a test row; no test row is ever trained on).
Ensemble   : 3 seeds, scores and reconstructions averaged.
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import time
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat
from phase2_step2_inbound_outbound_ai import CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer
from phase2_dense_anomaly_bank import LearnableGeometricAnomalyBank

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
N_IN = 502495
BLOCK = 12
N_PLS = 24
SEEDS = (11, 22, 33)


class CZETv2(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, zone_dim=72, d_model=192, nhead=4, num_layers=3, dim_ff=512,
                 bank_out=64, seq_len=38):
        super().__init__()
        self.core = CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer(zone_dim=zone_dim, d_model=d_model,
                                                 nhead=nhead, num_layers=num_layers,
                                                 dim_ff=dim_ff)
        self.bank = LearnableGeometricAnomalyBank(seq_len=seq_len, num_wavelets=16,
                                                  rqa_kernels=8, out_dim=bank_out)
        self.fuse = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(128 + bank_out * 4, d_model * 2),
            nn.GELU(), nn.LayerNorm(d_model * 2),
            nn.Linear(d_model * 2, 128),
        )
        self.pair = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(4 * 128 + 2, 256), nn.GELU(), nn.LayerNorm(256),
            nn.Linear(256, 64), nn.GELU(), nn.Linear(64, 1),
        )

    def forward(self, q, yq=None):
        p34, p12, z, anom = self.core(q)
        if yq is None:
            return p34, p12, z, anom
        b = torch.cat([self.bank(yq[:, i, :]) for i in range(4)], dim=-1)
        z = F.normalize(self.fuse(torch.cat([z, b], dim=-1)), dim=-1)
        return p34, p12, z, anom

    def score_pairs(self, zs, zy, cos, cov):
        return self.pair(torch.cat([zs, zy, zs * zy, (zs - zy).abs(),
                                    cos.unsqueeze(-1), cov.unsqueeze(-1)], dim=-1)).squeeze(-1)


def build():
    db = pqcompat.read_parquet(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_db.parquet"))
    ss = db[(db.covariant_peer_count >= 12) & (db.covariant_peer_count <= 240) &
            (db.mean_0_50_similarity >= 0.85) & (db.mean_0_50_similarity <= 0.955)]
    hub_ids = ss.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy(np.int64) - 1
    hub_ids = hub_ids[(hub_ids >= 0) & (hub_ids < N_IN)]
    edges = pqcompat.read_parquet(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_edges.parquet"))
    hset = set(hub_ids.tolist())
    m = edges.src_idx.isin(hset) & edges.dst_idx.isin(hset)
    e = edges[m & (edges.src_idx < N_IN) & (edges.dst_idx < N_IN)].reset_index(drop=True)
    ids = np.unique(np.concatenate([hub_ids, e.src_idx.to_numpy(np.int64),
                                    e.dst_idx.to_numpy(np.int64)]))
    ids = ids[(ids >= 0) & (ids < N_IN)]
    print(f"[*] rows={len(ids):,} hubs={len(hub_ids):,} edges={len(e):,}", flush=True)

    Q = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_train_cache.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    q = np.ascontiguousarray(Q["Q_seq"][ids])
    held = np.ascontiguousarray(Q["protected_held"][ids].astype(np.float32))
    ret = np.ascontiguousarray(Q["q4_net_return"][ids])
    z = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "jump10_phase2_subzones.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    yq = np.stack([np.ascontiguousarray(z[f"y_q{i}"][ids]) for i in range(1, 5)], axis=1)

    loc = {int(v): i for i, v in enumerate(ids)}
    es = np.array([loc[int(v)] for v in e.src_idx], dtype=np.int64)
    ed = np.array([loc[int(v)] for v in e.dst_idx], dtype=np.int64)
    sign = np.sign(e.cov_0_50.to_numpy(np.float32))
    cov = np.abs(e.cov_0_50.to_numpy(np.float32))
    lock = np.clip(e.lock_50_100.to_numpy(np.float32), -1, 1)

    n = len(ids)
    acc = np.zeros((n, q.shape[2] * 2))
    cnt = np.zeros(n)
    q2 = q[:, :2, :].astype(np.float64)
    for a, b in zip(es, ed):
        acc[a] += q2[b].ravel()
        acc[b] += q2[a].ravel()
        cnt[a] += 1
        cnt[b] += 1
    fam = np.divide(acc, np.maximum(cnt, 1)[:, None])
    mac = np.nan_to_num(np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "_macro_desc.npy"))[ids // BLOCK].astype(np.float64))
    Xp = np.concatenate([q2.reshape(n, -1), fam, mac], axis=1)
    print(f"[*] predictor block {Xp.shape}", flush=True)
    return ids, q, yq, held, ret, es, ed, sign, cov, lock, Xp


def fit_pls(X, Y, tr, kc=N_PLS):
    mu = X[tr].mean(0)
    sd = X[tr].std(0) + 1e-9
    Xs = (X - mu) / sd
    my = Y[tr].mean(0)
    Xr = Xs[tr].copy()
    Yr = (Y - my)[tr].copy()
    W, P, C = [], [], []
    for _ in range(kc):
        u = Yr[:, 0]
        w = Xr.T @ u
        w /= np.linalg.norm(w) + 1e-12
        t = Xr @ w
        pp = Xr.T @ t / (t @ t)
        cc = Yr.T @ t / (t @ t)
        Xr -= np.outer(t, pp)
        Yr -= np.outer(t, cc)
        W.append(w)
        P.append(pp)
        C.append(cc)
    Wm, Pm, Cm = np.column_stack(W), np.column_stack(P), np.column_stack(C)
    B = Wm @ np.linalg.pinv(Pm.T @ Wm) @ Cm.T
    return {"mu": mu, "sd": sd, "my": my, "B": B, "k": kc}


def pls_predict(pls, X):
    return ((X - pls["mu"]) / pls["sd"]) @ pls["B"] + pls["my"]


def train_one(seed, D, epochs1, epochs2, batch):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    np.random.seed(seed)
    tr_rows, te_rows, tr_e, te_e = D["tr_rows"], D["te_rows"], D["tr_e"], D["te_e"]
    Qt, Yt, Ht, Rt = D["Qt"], D["Yt"], D["Ht"], D["Rt"]
    model = CZETv2()
    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
    log = {"pass1": [], "pass2": []}

    steps = max(1, len(tr_rows) // batch)
    for ep in range(1, epochs1 + 1):
        p = tr_rows[np.random.permutation(len(tr_rows))]
        tot, t0 = 0.0, time.time()
        model.train()
        for s in range(steps):
            idx = np.sort(p[s * batch:(s + 1) * batch])
            bq, by, bh, br = Qt[idx], Yt[idx], Ht[idx], Rt[idx]
            opt.zero_grad()
            p34, p12, _, anom = model(bq, by)
            loss = (F.mse_loss(p34, bq[:, 2:4, :]) + F.mse_loss(p12, bq[:, 0:2, :])
                    + 0.25 * (F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(anom[:, 0], bh)
                              + F.smooth_l1_loss(anom[:, 2], br)))
            loss.backward()
            opt.step()
            tot += loss.item()
        log["pass1"].append({"seed": seed, "epoch": ep, "loss": round(tot / steps, 5),
                             "secs": round(time.time() - t0, 1)})
        print(f"    [s{seed}] pass1 {ep}/{epochs1} loss {tot / steps:.5f} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)

    opt2 = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=1.5e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)
    sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(opt2, T_max=epochs2, eta_min=2e-5)
    S, Dd = D["S"], D["D"]
    SG, LK, CV = D["SG"], D["LK"], D["CV"]
    steps2 = max(1, len(tr_e) // batch)
    for ep in range(1, epochs2 + 1):
        p = np.random.permutation(len(tr_e))
        tot, t0 = 0.0, time.time()
        model.train()
        for s in range(steps2):
            j = torch.from_numpy(p[s * batch:(s + 1) * batch])
            sg, lk, cv = SG[j], LK[j], CV[j]
            opt2.zero_grad()
            p34z, _, zz, _ = model(Qt[S[j]], Yt[S[j]])
            _, _, zy, _ = model(Qt[Dd[j]], Yt[Dd[j]])
            cos = F.cosine_similarity(zz, zy * sg[:, None], dim=-1)
            sc = model.score_pairs(zz, zy, cos, cv)
            loss = (F.mse_loss(sc, lk) + 0.5 * F.mse_loss(cos, lk)
                    + 0.5 * F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(4.0 * sc, (lk >= 0.65).float())
                    + torch.mean(torch.sigmoid(lk * 3.0)[:, None, None]
                                 * (p34z - Qt[S[j]][:, 2:4, :]) ** 2))
            dl = lk[:, None] - lk[None, :]
            order = sc[:, None] - sc[None, :]
            mask = dl > 0.25
            if bool(mask.any()):
                loss = loss + 0.75 * F.softplus(-order[mask]).mean()
            loss.backward()
            opt2.step()
            tot += loss.item()
        sched.step()
        log["pass2"].append({"seed": seed, "epoch": ep, "loss": round(tot / steps2, 5),
                             "secs": round(time.time() - t0, 1)})
        print(f"    [s{seed}] pass2 {ep}/{epochs2} loss {tot / steps2:.5f} ({time.time() - t0:.0f}s)", flush=True)

    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        p34, _, _, _ = model(Qt[tr_rows], Yt[tr_rows])
        Pnet_tr = p34.numpy().reshape(len(tr_rows), -1)
        p34, _, _, _ = model(Qt[te_rows], Yt[te_rows])
        Pnet_te = p34.numpy().reshape(len(te_rows), -1)

        def scores(e_idx):
            zs_ = D["Qt"][D["es"][e_idx]]
            zy_ = D["Qt"][D["ed"][e_idx]]
            _, _, zz_, _ = model(zs_, D["Yt"][D["es"][e_idx]])
            _, _, zy2_, _ = model(zy_, D["Yt"][D["ed"][e_idx]])
            cs_ = F.cosine_similarity(zz_, zy2_ * torch.from_numpy(D["sign"][e_idx])[:, None], dim=-1)
            return model.score_pairs(zz_, zy2_, cs_, torch.from_numpy(D["cov"][e_idx])).numpy()

        s_tr = scores(tr_e)
        s_te = scores(te_e)
    return model, log, Pnet_tr, Pnet_te, s_tr, s_te


def main(epochs1=8, epochs2=30, batch=128, threads=4):
    torch.set_num_threads(threads)
    ids, q, yq, held, ret, es, ed, sign, cov, lock, Xp = build()

    n = len(ids)
    perm = np.random.default_rng(11).permutation(n)
    n_tr = int(0.8 * n)
    tr_rows = np.sort(perm[:n_tr])
    te_rows = np.sort(perm[n_tr:])
    tr_set = np.zeros(n, bool)
    tr_set[tr_rows] = True
    te_set = ~tr_set
    tr_e = np.where(tr_set[es] & tr_set[ed])[0]
    te_e = np.where(te_set[es] | te_set[ed])[0]
    print(f"[*] train rows={len(tr_rows):,} test rows={len(te_rows):,} "
          f"train edges={len(tr_e):,} test edges={len(te_e):,}", flush=True)

    Y = q[:, 2:4, :].reshape(n, -1).astype(np.float64)
    pls = fit_pls(Xp, Y, tr_rows)
    np.savez(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_phase2_pls.npz"),
             mu=pls["mu"], sd=pls["sd"], my=pls["my"], B=pls["B"], k=np.array([pls["k"]]))
    Ppls_tr = pls_predict(pls, Xp[tr_rows])
    Ppls_te = pls_predict(pls, Xp[te_rows])
    r2_pls = float(1 - ((Ppls_te - Y[te_rows]) ** 2).sum() /
                   ((Y[te_rows] - Y[te_rows].mean(0)) ** 2).sum())
    print(f"[*] PLS({pls['k']}) held-out R2 = {r2_pls:.5f}", flush=True)

    D = {"tr_rows": tr_rows, "te_rows": te_rows, "tr_e": tr_e, "te_e": te_e,
         "Qt": torch.from_numpy(q), "Yt": torch.from_numpy(yq),
         "Ht": torch.from_numpy(held), "Rt": torch.from_numpy(ret),
         "es": es, "ed": ed, "sign": sign, "cov": cov, "lock": lock,
         "S": torch.from_numpy(es[tr_e]), "D": torch.from_numpy(ed[tr_e]),
         "SG": torch.from_numpy(sign[tr_e]), "LK": torch.from_numpy(lock[tr_e]),
         "CV": torch.from_numpy(cov[tr_e])}

    logs, Ptr, Pte, Str, Ste, models = [], [], [], [], [], []
    for sd in SEEDS:
        m, lg, a, b, c, d = train_one(sd, D, epochs1, epochs2, batch)
        models.append(m)
        logs.append(lg)
        Ptr.append(a)
        Pte.append(b)
        Str.append(c)
        Ste.append(d)
        from scipy.stats import spearmanr
        print(f"    [s{sd}] seed held-out Spearman = "
              f"{float(spearmanr(d, lock[te_e]).statistic):.4f}", flush=True)

    Pnet_tr = np.mean(Ptr, axis=0)
    Pnet_te = np.mean(Pte, axis=0)
    s_te = np.mean(Ste, axis=0)
    s_tr = np.mean(Str, axis=0)
    Ytr, Yte = Y[tr_rows], Y[te_rows]

    num = float(np.sum((Pnet_tr - Ppls_tr) * (Ytr - Ppls_tr)))
    den = float(np.sum((Pnet_tr - Ppls_tr) ** 2)) + 1e-12
    lam = float(np.clip(num / den, 0.0, 1.0))
    w = np.array([lam, 1.0 - lam])
    Pst_te = w[0] * Pnet_te + w[1] * Ppls_te
    r2_net = float(1 - ((Pnet_te - Yte) ** 2).sum() / ((Yte - Yte.mean(0)) ** 2).sum())
    r2_st = float(1 - ((Pst_te - Yte) ** 2).sum() / ((Yte - Yte.mean(0)) ** 2).sum())

    lk_te = lock[te_e]
    from scipy.stats import spearmanr
    rho = float(spearmanr(s_te, lk_te).statistic)
    pear = float(np.corrcoef(s_te, lk_te)[0, 1])
    pos = lk_te >= 0.65
    prec = None
    if pos.sum() > 5:
        k10 = max(1, int(0.1 * len(s_te)))
        prec = float(pos[np.argsort(-s_te)[:k10]].mean())
    rho_cov = float(spearmanr(cov[te_e], lk_te).statistic)

    out = {
        "model": "CZETv2 + LearnableGeometricAnomalyBank + pair head + PLS path (3-seed ensemble)",
        "universe": "sweet spot: 12-240 peers, 0.85-0.955 mean|r|",
        "train_rows": int(len(tr_rows)), "test_rows": int(len(te_rows)),
        "train_edges": int(len(tr_e)), "test_edges": int(len(te_e)),
        "spearman": round(rho, 4), "pair_spearman": round(rho, 4),
        "spearman_observed_cov_baseline": round(rho_cov, 4),
        "r2": round(r2_st, 5), "oos_r2": round(r2_st, 5),
        "r2_net_only": round(r2_net, 5), "r2_pls_only": round(r2_pls, 5),
        "stack_weights": {"net": round(float(w[0]), 4), "pls": round(float(w[1]), 4)},
        "pair_score_vs_lock_pearson": round(pear, 4),
        "precision_at_10pct": None if prec is None else round(prec, 4),
        "base_rate_lock_ge_065": round(float(pos.mean()), 4),
        "params": int(sum(p.numel() for p in models[0].parameters())),
        "seeds": list(SEEDS), "epochs": {"pass1": epochs1, "pass2": epochs2},
        "protocol": "rows split 80/20 at random; test edges are those touching a test row; "
                    "PLS, stack weight and pair head are fitted on train only; R2 on held-out rows.",
    }
    print(json.dumps(out, indent=2), flush=True)
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_eval.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_train_log.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump({"runs": logs, "config": {"d_model": 192, "layers": 3, "bank_out": 64,
                                            "pls_components": N_PLS, "lr_pass1": 3e-4,
                                            "lr_pass2": 1.5e-4, "batch": batch, "seeds": list(SEEDS),
                                            "universe": "sweetspot 12-240 peers, 0.85-0.955 |r|"}}, f, indent=2)
    torch.save({"state_dicts": [m.state_dict() for m in models],
                "cfg": {"zone_dim": 72, "d_model": 192, "nhead": 4, "num_layers": 3,
                        "dim_ff": 512, "bank_out": 64, "seq_len": 38},
                "stack_weights": w.tolist(), "seeds": list(SEEDS)},
               os.path.join(BASE, "czet_phase2_sweetspot.pt"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_czet_eval.py - held-out evaluation of the pair head (72 lines, 2.8 KB)
"""Out-of-sample evaluation of the trained CZET (runs on the X Flip, CPU-light)."""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np
import torch

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
from phase2_step2_inbound_outbound_ai import CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer, read_table

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"


def main(n_pairs=8000, n_rows=4000, seed=7):
    np.random.seed(seed)
    ck = torch.load(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_phase2.pt"), map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    cfg = ck["cfg"]
    model = CrossZoneEpicycleTransformer(**cfg)
    model.load_state_dict(ck["state_dict"])
    model.eval()
    print("[*] loaded", cfg)

    cache = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_train_cache.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    Q = cache["Q_seq"]
    N = Q.shape[0]
    n_in = int(N * 0.85)
    out = {"cfg": cfg, "train_rows": 150000}

    # 1) out-of-sample quartile reconstruction (last 15% never trained)
    idx = np.random.permutation(np.arange(n_in, N))[:n_rows]
    idx.sort()
    q = torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[idx]))
    with torch.no_grad():
        p34, p12, z, anom = model(q)
    for tag, pred, tgt in (("q34", p34, q[:, 2:4, :]), ("q12", p12, q[:, 0:2, :])):
        sse = float(((pred - tgt) ** 2).sum())
        sst = float(((tgt - tgt.mean(0, keepdim=True)) ** 2).sum())
        out[f"{tag}_oos_r2"] = round(1 - sse / sst, 5)
        out[f"{tag}_oos_mse"] = round(float(((pred - tgt) ** 2).mean()), 6)

    # 2) covariant metric quality on labelled pairs
    edges = read_table(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_edges.parquet"))
    e = edges.sample(n=min(n_pairs, len(edges)), random_state=seed)
    src = e["src_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    dst = e["dst_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
    sign = np.sign(e["cov_0_50"].to_numpy(np.float32))
    lock = e["lock_50_100"].to_numpy(np.float32)
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, _, zs, _ = model(torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[src])))
        _, _, zd, _ = model(torch.from_numpy(np.ascontiguousarray(Q[dst])))
    cos = (zs * (zd * torch.from_numpy(sign)[:, None])).sum(-1).numpy()
    lockc = np.clip(lock, -1, 1)
    out["pairs"] = int(len(e))
    out["pair_cos_vs_lock_pearson"] = round(float(np.corrcoef(cos, lockc)[0, 1]), 4)
    if len(cos) > 2:
        from scipy.stats import spearmanr
        out["pair_cos_vs_lock_spearman"] = round(float(spearmanr(cos, lockc).statistic), 4)
    pos = lockc >= 0.65
    if pos.sum() and (~pos).sum():
        order = np.argsort(-cos)
        hits = pos[order]
        tp = np.cumsum(hits)
        prec = tp / np.arange(1, len(hits) + 1)
        out["precision_at_10pct"] = round(float(prec[max(0, int(0.1 * len(hits)) - 1)]), 4)
        out["base_rate_lock_ge_065"] = round(float(pos.mean()), 4)
    print(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "czet_eval.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_null_test.py - selection-aware block permutation test (106 lines, 4.3 KB)
"""Selection-aware block-permutation null test for the Phase-2 vault (item 10).

The naive per-strategy p-value is meaningless here because the 893 strategies were
*selected* on the outbound window. This script therefore re-selects the top-300 in
every permutation and compares the selected-set mean win rate, which is the only
honest test of "the vault beats a block-shuffled null".

Outputs: null_test.json, phase2_strategy_vault.parquet (null columns updated)
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
N, N_IN = 591171, 502495
NPERM = 400
TOP = 300


def wilson(p, n, z=1.959964):
    p = np.clip(p, 0, 1)
    d = 1 + z ** 2 / n
    c = p + z ** 2 / (2 * n)
    r = z * np.sqrt(p * (1 - p) / n + z ** 2 / (4 * n ** 2))
    return (c - r) / d


def main():
    tg = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "vault_triggers.npz"))
    idx_all, pol_all, off = tg["idx"], tg["pol"], tg["off"]
    scan = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "jump10_phase2_scan.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    held_o = np.asarray(scan["protected_held"])[N_IN:].astype(np.float32)
    ret_o = np.asarray(scan["q4_net_return"])[N_IN:].astype(np.float32)
    nb = len(held_o)
    n_full = nb // 150
    tail = np.arange(n_full * 150, nb, dtype=np.int64)
    base = np.arange(n_full)[:, None] * 150 + np.arange(150)[None, :]
    lis = [idx_all[off[i]:off[i + 1]] - N_IN for i in range(len(off) - 1)]
    pols = [pol_all[off[i]:off[i + 1]] for i in range(len(off) - 1)]
    ns = np.array([len(x) for x in lis], dtype=float)
    print(f"[*] {len(lis)} strategies, triggers {int(ns.sum()):,}", flush=True)

    def win_arrays(h, r):
        w = np.empty(len(lis), dtype=np.float64)
        for i, (li, po) in enumerate(zip(lis, pols)):
            w[i] = max(float(np.mean(h[li])), float(np.mean(np.sign(r[li] * po) == 1)))
        return w

    obs = win_arrays(held_o, ret_o)
    obs_lb = wilson(obs, ns)
    obs_sel = np.argsort(-obs_lb)[:TOP]
    obs_mean = float(obs[obs_sel].mean())
    obs_pool_n = int(ns[obs_sel].sum())

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20261025)
    null_mean = np.empty(NPERM)
    null_pool = np.empty(NPERM)
    for p in range(NPERM):
        pm = rng.permutation(n_full)
        pos = np.concatenate([(pm[:, None] * 150 + np.arange(150)[None, :]).ravel(), tail])
        w = win_arrays(held_o[pos], ret_o[pos])
        sel = np.argsort(-wilson(w, ns))[:TOP]
        null_mean[p] = w[sel].mean()
        null_pool[p] = np.sum(w[sel] * ns[sel]) / ns[sel].sum() if ns[sel].sum() else 0
        if (p + 1) % 100 == 0:
            print(f"    perm {p + 1}/{NPERM} | null top300 mean {null_mean[p]:.4f}", flush=True)

    p_agg = float((np.sum(null_mean >= obs_mean) + 1) / (NPERM + 1))
    obs_pool = float(np.sum(obs[obs_sel] * ns[obs_sel]) / obs_pool_n)
    p_pool = float((np.sum(null_pool >= obs_pool) + 1) / (NPERM + 1))
    per_p = np.array([float((np.sum(np.abs(null_mean - null_mean.mean()) >= abs(obs[i] - null_mean.mean())) + 1) / (NPERM + 1))
                      for i in range(len(lis))])

    out = {
        "permutations": NPERM, "block_size_ticks": 150, "top": TOP,
        "observed_top300_mean_win": round(obs_mean, 4),
        "null_top300_mean_median": round(float(np.median(null_mean)), 4),
        "null_top300_mean_p99": round(float(np.quantile(null_mean, 0.99)), 4),
        "p_selection_aware": round(p_agg, 4),
        "observed_pooled_win": round(obs_pool, 4),
        "null_pooled_median": round(float(np.median(null_pool)), 4),
        "p_pooled": round(p_pool, 4),
        "beats_null_p01": bool(p_agg < 0.01),
        "verdict": ("vault beats the block-permutation null" if p_agg < 0.01
                    else "OUTBOUND EDGE IS NOT DISTINGUISHABLE FROM A BLOCK-SHUFFLED NULL "
                         "(selection on the outbound window is the likely cause)"),
    }
    print(json.dumps(out, indent=2), flush=True)
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "null_test.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(out, f, indent=2)

    v = pqcompat.read_parquet(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))
    v["null_pval"] = np.round(per_p, 4)
    v["null_pval_selection_aware"] = round(p_agg, 4)
    v["beats_null"] = bool(p_agg < 0.01)
    v["null_verdict"] = out["verdict"]
    pqcompat.write_parquet(v, os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

Note what the permutation loop permutes: the outcome. Note what it does not permute: the selection rule. This is the design decision discussed in Chapters 10 and 15.

phase2/phase2_fractal_fix.py - multi-timeframe consistency test (86 lines, 4.0 KB)
"""Recompute the macro/micro fractal agreement in a standardized descriptor space.

The first pass correlated raw 64-d descriptors, whose scale-dependent terms (s_y,
int_y_dt, quad_var ...) carry the absolute price level and forced every pair to ~1.
Here both vectors are z-scored per feature against the macro population first, so the
agreement measures *shape*, not price level.
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat
from phase2_dense_anomaly_bank import compute_quartile_brute_force_features

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
P1 = r"C:\cluster\phase1\final\jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz"
BLOCK = 12


def main():
    vault = pqcompat.read_parquet(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))
    macro = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "_macro_desc.npy"))
    print("[*] macro descriptors", macro.shape, flush=True)

    px = np.load(P1, mmap_mode="r")["raw_tick_cells"]
    hub_idx = vault.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy(np.int64) - 1
    micro_paths = np.empty((len(hub_idx), 150), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(0, len(hub_idx), 2000):
        micro_paths[i:i + 2000] = np.asarray(px[hub_idx[i:i + 2000]], dtype=np.float64)
    micro = compute_quartile_brute_force_features(micro_paths)
    np.save(os.path.join(BASE, "_micro_desc_hubs.npy"), micro.astype(np.float32))

    mu = macro.mean(0, keepdims=True)
    sd = macro.std(0, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
    Zm = np.clip((macro - mu) / sd, -6, 6)
    Zi = np.clip((micro - mu) / sd, -6, 6)
    blocks = hub_idx // BLOCK
    Mb = Zm[blocks]
    Zi_c = Zi - Zi.mean(1, keepdims=True)
    Mb_c = Mb - Mb.mean(1, keepdims=True)
    agree = np.sum(Zi_c * Mb_c, axis=1) / (
        np.sqrt(np.sum(Zi_c ** 2, axis=1) * np.sum(Mb_c ** 2, axis=1)) + 1e-12)

    # scale-free path shape agreement: z-scored 150-point paths
    mp = micro_paths - micro_paths.mean(1, keepdims=True)
    mp /= (mp.std(1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
    macro_paths = np.empty((len(set(blocks.tolist())), 150))
    pos = {b: i for i, b in enumerate(sorted(set(blocks.tolist())))}
    for i, b in enumerate(sorted(pos)):
        s = b * BLOCK
        e = min(len(px), s + BLOCK)
        blk = np.asarray(px[s:e], dtype=np.float64)
        if blk.shape[0] < BLOCK:
            blk = np.vstack([blk, np.repeat(blk[-1:], BLOCK - blk.shape[0], axis=0)])
        macro_paths[i] = blk.reshape(BLOCK, 150).mean(0)
    macro_paths -= macro_paths.mean(1, keepdims=True)
    macro_paths /= (macro_paths.std(1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
    mpb = macro_paths[[pos[b] for b in blocks.tolist()]]
    shape = np.sum(mp * mpb, axis=1) / 150.0

    q90 = float(np.quantile(agree, 0.90))
    vault["fractal_macro_agreement"] = np.round(agree, 4)
    vault["fractal_shape_agreement"] = np.round(shape, 4)
    vault["is_fractal_anomaly"] = agree >= q90
    pqcompat.write_parquet(vault, os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))

    rep = {
        "descriptor_agreement": {"min": round(float(agree.min()), 4), "median": round(float(np.median(agree)), 4),
                                 "p75": round(float(np.quantile(agree, .75)), 4), "max": round(float(agree.max()), 4),
                                 "std": round(float(agree.std()), 4), "p90_threshold": round(q90, 4)},
        "path_shape_agreement": {"median": round(float(np.median(shape)), 4),
                                 "p90": round(float(np.quantile(shape, .9)), 4)},
        "is_fractal_anomalies": int((agree >= q90).sum()),
        "agreement_vs_verified_edge": round(float(np.corrcoef(agree, vault.outbound_verified_edge.to_numpy(float))[0, 1]), 4),
        "fractal_anomalies_mean_edge": round(float(vault.loc[agree >= q90, "outbound_verified_edge"].mean()), 4),
        "non_fractal_mean_edge": round(float(vault.loc[agree < q90, "outbound_verified_edge"].mean()), 4),
    }
    print(json.dumps(rep, indent=2), flush=True)
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "fractal_report.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(rep, f, indent=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_crawled_operators.py - nine complexity operators (169 lines, 7.9 KB)
"""Item 03 - crawled mathematical trajectory operators.

Computes the literature-derived invariants that were NOT in the original 12-angle
brute-force bank, over the strictly causal 0-50% window of every cell, and writes
crawled_math_operators.npz + crawled_math_operators_manifest.json.

Operators
  HVG degree entropy + entropy gap vs the iid-exponential law  (Luque et al., PRE 80, 046103)
  Hilbert-Huang instantaneous frequency mean/std               (Huang et al., Proc. R. Soc. A 454, 903)
  Normalized Levy area of the (t, y) path                      (Chevyrev & Kormilitzin, arXiv:1603.03788, eq. 12)
  RQA determinism / laminarity                                 (Marwan et al., Phys. Rep. 438, 237)
  Ordinal permutation entropy                                  (Bandt & Pompe, PRL 88, 174102)
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat
from scipy.signal import hilbert
from phase2_dense_anomaly_bank import _rqa_features, _ordinal_features

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
P1 = r"C:\cluster\phase1\final\jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz"
N = 591171
WIN = 75
CHUNK = 4000

K = np.arange(2, 200)
P_TH = (1.0 / 3.0) * (2.0 / 3.0) ** (K - 2)
H_TH = float(-np.sum(P_TH * np.log2(P_TH)))


def hvg_degree(y):
    """Exact horizontal-visibility degree (all lags) via next/prev greater-or-equal."""
    n, L = y.shape
    i = np.arange(L)
    upper = np.triu(np.ones((L, L), dtype=bool), 1)[None]
    lower = np.tril(np.ones((L, L), dtype=bool), -1)[None]
    nxt = (y[:, None, :] >= y[:, :, None]) & upper
    prv = (y[:, :, None] >= y[:, None, :]) & lower
    has_n = nxt.any(2)
    has_p = prv.any(2)
    jn = np.argmax(nxt, axis=2)
    jp = L - 1 - np.argmax(prv[:, :, ::-1], axis=2)
    deg = (jn - i[None, :] - 1) + (i[None, :] - jp - 1) + has_n.astype(np.int32) + has_p.astype(np.int32)
    return np.clip(deg, 0, L - 1).astype(np.float32)


def hvg_entropy(deg):
    n, length = deg.shape
    hist = np.zeros((n, length + 1), dtype=np.float32)
    rows = np.repeat(np.arange(n), length)
    np.add.at(hist, (rows, deg.astype(np.int32).ravel()), 1.0)
    p = hist / np.maximum(hist.sum(1, keepdims=True), 1e-9)
    return -np.sum(np.where(p > 0, p * np.log2(np.where(p > 0, p, 1)), 0.0), axis=1)


def main():
    npz_out = os.path.join(BASE, "crawled_math_operators.npz")
    px = np.load(P1, mmap_mode="r")["raw_tick_cells"]
    names = ["hvg_degree_entropy", "hvg_entropy_gap", "hvg_max_degree",
             "hh_inst_freq_mean", "hh_inst_freq_std",
             "levy_area_z", "rqa_det", "rqa_lam", "perm_entropy"]
    arrs = {}
    cached = os.path.exists(npz_out) and "--force" not in sys.argv
    if cached:
        d = np.load(npz_out)
        arrs = {n: d[n] for n in names}
        print("[*] using cached crawled_math_operators.npz", flush=True)
    for n in names:
        if not cached:
            arrs[n] = np.lib.format.open_memmap(os.path.join(BASE, f"_op_{n}.npy"), mode="w+",
                                                dtype=np.float32, shape=(N,))
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, WIN)
    for s in ([] if cached else range(0, N, CHUNK)):
        e = min(N, s + CHUNK)
        p = np.asarray(px[s:e, :WIN], dtype=np.float64)
        y = p - p.mean(1, keepdims=True)
        y = y / (y.std(1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
        dy = np.diff(y, axis=1)
        dt = 1.0 / (WIN - 1)
        area = 0.5 * (np.sum(t[:-1] * dy, axis=1) - np.sum(y[:, :-1] * dt, axis=1))
        arrs["levy_area_z"][s:e] = area / (np.abs(dy).sum(1) + 1e-9)

        ana = hilbert(y, axis=1)
        inst = np.diff(np.unwrap(np.angle(ana), axis=1), axis=1) / (2 * np.pi * dt)
        arrs["hh_inst_freq_mean"][s:e] = inst.mean(1) / 100.0
        arrs["hh_inst_freq_std"][s:e] = inst.std(1) / 100.0

        rqa, _ = _rqa_features(y)
        arrs["rqa_det"][s:e] = rqa[:, 1]
        arrs["rqa_lam"][s:e] = rqa[:, 2]

        ord_feat = _ordinal_features(y)
        arrs["perm_entropy"][s:e] = ord_feat[:, 6]

        deg = hvg_degree(y)
        h = hvg_entropy(deg)
        arrs["hvg_degree_entropy"][s:e] = h
        arrs["hvg_entropy_gap"][s:e] = h - H_TH
        arrs["hvg_max_degree"][s:e] = deg.max(1)
        print(f"    operators {e:,}/{N:,}", flush=True)

    if not cached:
        for n in names:
            arrs[n].flush()
        np.savez(npz_out, **{n: np.asarray(arrs[n]) for n in names})

    vault = pqcompat.read_parquet(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))
    ai = vault.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy(np.int64) - 1
    assoc = {}
    for n in names:
        v = np.asarray(arrs[n])[ai]
        assoc[n] = {
            "min": round(float(np.nanmin(v)), 5), "median": round(float(np.nanmedian(v)), 5),
            "max": round(float(np.nanmax(v)), 5),
            "corr_vs_outbound_edge": round(float(np.corrcoef(v, vault.outbound_verified_edge.to_numpy(float))[0, 1]), 4),
        }
        ptmp = os.path.join(BASE, f"_op_{n}.npy")
        if os.path.exists(ptmp):
            try:
                os.remove(ptmp)
            except Exception:
                pass

    manifest = {
        "generated": "2026-10-25",
        "purpose": "Literature-sourced trajectory invariants crawled for Phase 2 item 03 and computed on the "
                   "causal 0-50% window (ticks 1..75) of all 591,171 cells.",
        "window": {"start_tick": 1, "end_tick": 75, "length": WIN,
                   "preprocessing": "per-window mean-centering and unit-variance scaling (df=74)"},
        "operators": [
            {"name": "hvg_degree_entropy", "family": "complex networks",
             "formula": "H(d) = -sum_k p_k log2 p_k over the horizontal-visibility-graph degree distribution",
             "reference": "Luque, Lacasa, Ballesteros & Luque, Phys. Rev. E 80, 046103 (2009), doi:10.1103/PhysRevE.80.046103",
             "note": "iid series produce P(k)=(1/3)(2/3)^(k-2) regardless of amplitude law"},
            {"name": "hvg_entropy_gap", "family": "complex networks",
             "formula": "H_empirical - H_theory with H_theory = %.6f bits for the iid exponential law" % H_TH,
             "reference": "same as hvg_degree_entropy", "note": "positive gap = excess order vs white noise"},
            {"name": "hvg_max_degree", "family": "complex networks", "formula": "max_i deg(i)",
             "reference": "same as hvg_degree_entropy"},
            {"name": "hh_inst_freq_mean", "family": "Hilbert-Huang",
             "formula": "mean of d/dt arg(Hilbert(y)) / 2*pi", "reference": "Huang et al., Proc. R. Soc. A 454, 903 (1998)"},
            {"name": "hh_inst_freq_std", "family": "Hilbert-Huang",
             "formula": "std of the instantaneous frequency", "reference": "same as hh_inst_freq_mean"},
            {"name": "levy_area_z", "family": "rough paths",
             "formula": "A = 0.5*(int t dy - int y dt), normalized by total variation",
             "reference": "Chevyrev & Kormilitzin, arXiv:1603.03788 eq. (12); Lyons (1998) rough path theory"},
            {"name": "rqa_det", "family": "recurrence quantification",
             "formula": "DET = sum(diagonal recurrence lengths >= 2) / total recurrence (16-pt delay embedding)",
             "reference": "Marwan, Romano, Thiel & Kurths, Phys. Rep. 438, 237 (2007)"},
            {"name": "rqa_lam", "family": "recurrence quantification",
             "formula": "LAM = vertical-line recurrence / total recurrence", "reference": "same as rqa_det"},
            {"name": "perm_entropy", "family": "ordinal statistics",
             "formula": "H = -sum p(pi) ln p(pi) / ln 6 over the 6 ordinal patterns of order 3",
             "reference": "Bandt & Pompe, Phys. Rev. Lett. 88, 174102 (2002)"},
        ],
        "arrays": {"path": "crawled_math_operators.npz", "rows": N, "dtype": "float32", "columns": names},
        "vault_association": assoc,
    }
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "crawled_math_operators_manifest.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(manifest, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps({"H_theory": round(H_TH, 4), "association": assoc}, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_merge_db.py - statement database assembly (154 lines, 7.0 KB)
"""Merge the 3 node shards into the Phase-2 Relational Covariant Database.

Runs on the X Flip (cheap: only the hub/edge tables move, never the matrices).
Produces:
  C:\\cluster\\phase2\\phase2_covariant_db.parquet
  C:\\cluster\\phase2\\phase2_covariant_edges.parquet
  C:\\cluster\\phase2\\phase2_covariant_summary.json
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import glob
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, r"C:\cluster\phase2")
import pqcompat

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
SHARDS = os.path.join(BASE, "shards")
RECV = r"C:\cluster\phase1\out\phase2\shards_received"
N_TOTAL = 591171

COLS = ["anchor_cell_id", "covariant_peer_count", "peers_evaluated",
        "pos_corr_peers", "neg_mirror_peers", "mean_0_50_similarity",
        "max_0_50_similarity", "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "frac_75_100_exact_lock",
        "protected_ext_win_rate", "directional_50_100_win_rate",
        "direction_consistency", "anomaly_z_score", "bicoherence_phase_lock",
        "target_ext_tick_median", "recommended_action"]


def load_any(path):
    if path.endswith(".parquet"):
        return pqcompat.read_parquet(path)
    df = pd.read_csv(path)
    if "recommended_action" not in df.columns:
        return df
    return df


def main():
    node_files = {}
    for ext in ("parquet", "csv"):
        for pat in (os.path.join(SHARDS, f"covariant_anomalies_node?.{ext}"),
                    os.path.join(RECV, f"covariant_anomalies_node?.{ext}")):
            for f in sorted(glob.glob(pat)):
                key = os.path.basename(f).split("_node")[1].split(".")[0]
                node_files.setdefault(key, f)
    if not node_files:
        print("[-] no shard files found", flush=True)
        sys.exit(1)
    print("[*] hub shards:", node_files, flush=True)

    parts = []
    for k, f in sorted(node_files.items()):
        df = load_any(f)
        df["shard"] = int(k)
        parts.append(df)
        print(f"    node {k}: {len(df):,} anomaly hubs  <- {f}", flush=True)
    db = pd.concat(parts, ignore_index=True)
    before = len(db)
    db = db.sort_values("anomaly_z_score", ascending=False)
    db = db.drop_duplicates(subset="anchor_cell_id", keep="first").reset_index(drop=True)
    print(f"[*] merged {before:,} -> {len(db):,} unique anomaly hubs", flush=True)

    for c in COLS:
        if c not in db.columns:
            db[c] = np.nan
    db = db[COLS + ["shard"]]

    all_anchors = set(db["anchor_cell_id"].astype(np.int64))
    missing = N_TOTAL - len(all_anchors)
    out_db = os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_db.parquet")
    pqcompat.write_parquet(db, out_db)
    print(f"[+] {out_db}  ({os.path.getsize(out_db)/1e6:.2f} MB)", flush=True)

    # ---- edges ------------------------------------------------------------
    eparts = []
    for k in sorted(node_files):
        for ext in ("parquet", "csv"):
            for pat in (os.path.join(SHARDS, f"covariant_edges_node{k}.{ext}"),
                        os.path.join(RECV, f"covariant_edges_node{k}.{ext}")):
                for f in glob.glob(pat):
                    e = load_any(f)
                    if len(e):
                        eparts.append(e)
    if eparts:
        edges = pd.concat(eparts, ignore_index=True)
        edges["abs_cov"] = edges["cov_0_50"].abs()
        edges = edges.sort_values("abs_cov", ascending=False)
        s = edges["src_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        d = edges["dst_idx"].to_numpy(np.int64)
        edges["pair"] = np.minimum(s, d) * np.int64(N_TOTAL + 1) + np.maximum(s, d)
        edges = edges.drop_duplicates(subset="pair", keep="first").drop(columns=["pair", "abs_cov"])
        out_e = os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_edges.parquet")
        pqcompat.write_parquet(edges, out_e)
        n_edges = len(edges)
        print(f"[+] {out_e}  ({n_edges:,} unique undirected edges, {os.path.getsize(out_e)/1e6:.2f} MB)", flush=True)
    else:
        n_edges = 0
        edges = pd.DataFrame(columns=["src_idx", "dst_idx", "cov_0_50", "lock_50_100"])

    # ---- summary ----------------------------------------------------------
    top = db.head(25)
    summary = {
        "cells_total": N_TOTAL,
        "anomaly_hubs": int(len(db)),
        "hub_rate_pct": round(100.0 * len(db) / N_TOTAL, 3),
        "cells_not_anomalous": int(missing),
        "unique_undirected_edges": int(n_edges),
        "shards": {k: int(len(p)) for k, p in zip(sorted(node_files), parts)},
        "covariant_similarity": {
            "max_single_peer_abs_r": round(float(db["max_0_50_similarity"].max()), 4),
            "max_single_peer_abs_r_anchor": int(db.loc[db["max_0_50_similarity"].idxmax(), "anchor_cell_id"]),
            "median_mean_abs_r": round(float(db["mean_0_50_similarity"].median()), 4),
            "p99_mean_abs_r": round(float(np.percentile(db["mean_0_50_similarity"], 99)), 4),
        },
        "continuation_lock": {
            "max_mean_lock_corr": round(float(db["mean_50_100_lock_corr"].max()), 4),
            "max_mean_lock_corr_anchor": int(db.loc[db["mean_50_100_lock_corr"].idxmax(), "anchor_cell_id"]),
            "max_frac_exact_lock": round(float(db["frac_75_100_exact_lock"].max()), 4),
            "median_mean_lock_corr": round(float(db["mean_50_100_lock_corr"].median()), 4),
        },
        "edge_quality": {
            "max_protected_ext_win_rate": round(float(db["protected_ext_win_rate"].max()), 4),
            "median_protected_ext_win_rate": round(float(db["protected_ext_win_rate"].median()), 4),
            "max_directional_win_rate": round(float(db["directional_50_100_win_rate"].max()), 4),
            "median_directional_win_rate": round(float(db["directional_50_100_win_rate"].median()), 4),
            "max_anomaly_z_score": round(float(db["anomaly_z_score"].max()), 3),
            "hubs_z_ge_3": int((db["anomaly_z_score"] >= 3).sum()),
            "hubs_protected_win_ge_60pct": int((db["protected_ext_win_rate"] >= 0.60).sum()),
            "hubs_directional_win_ge_60pct": int((db["directional_50_100_win_rate"] >= 0.60).sum()),
        },
        "peer_counts": {
            "median": int(db["covariant_peer_count"].median()),
            "p90": int(np.percentile(db["covariant_peer_count"], 90)),
            "max": int(db["covariant_peer_count"].max()),
            "median_evaluated": int(db["peers_evaluated"].median()),
        },
        "actions": db["recommended_action"].value_counts().to_dict(),
        "mean_ext_tick": round(float(db["target_ext_tick_median"].mean()), 1),
        "top_10_hubs": top.head(10)[["anchor_cell_id", "covariant_peer_count",
                                    "mean_0_50_similarity", "max_0_50_similarity",
                                    "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "protected_ext_win_rate",
                                    "directional_50_100_win_rate", "anomaly_z_score",
                                    "recommended_action"]].to_dict(orient="records"),
    }
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_summary.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_report.py - eleven-sheet workbook writer (179 lines, 9.0 KB)
"""Phase 2 anomaly-efficiency report (X Flip, light).

Inputs : phase2_covariant_db.parquet, phase2_covariant_edges.parquet,
         phase2_covariant_summary.json, phase2_strategy_vault.(parquet|csv)
Outputs: phase2_anomaly_efficiency_report.json
         phase2_anomaly_efficiency_report.xlsx
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"


def read_table(p):
    return pqcompat.read_parquet(p) if p.endswith(".parquet") else pd.read_csv(p)


def main():
    db = read_table(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_db.parquet"))
    edges = read_table(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_edges.parquet"))
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_summary.json")) as f:
        summ = json.load(f)
    vault = None
    for n in ("phase2_strategy_vault.parquet", "phase2_strategy_vault.csv"):
        p = os.path.join(BASE, n)
        if os.path.exists(p):
            vault = read_table(p)
            break

    n = db["covariant_peer_count"].clip(lower=1).to_numpy(float)
    for col, label in (("protected_ext_win_rate", "prot"), ("directional_50_100_win_rate", "dir")):
        p = db[col].to_numpy(float)
        db[f"{label}_edge"] = p - 0.5
        db[f"{label}_z"] = (p - 0.5) / np.sqrt(0.25 / n)
        db[f"{label}_p"] = 0.5 * np.vectorize(lambda z: __import__("math").erfc(abs(z) / np.sqrt(2)))(db[f"{label}_z"])

    db["best_win"] = db[["protected_ext_win_rate", "directional_50_100_win_rate"]].max(axis=1)
    db["best_edge"] = db["best_win"] - 0.5
    db["best_z"] = (db["best_win"] - 0.5) / np.sqrt(0.25 / n)
    db["efficiency_score"] = db["best_z"] * np.sqrt(n) / 100.0

    q = [0, .01, .05, .25, .5, .75, .95, .99, 1.0]
    dist = {}
    for col in ("covariant_peer_count", "mean_0_50_similarity", "max_0_50_similarity",
                "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "frac_75_100_exact_lock", "protected_ext_win_rate",
                "directional_50_100_win_rate", "anomaly_z_score", "best_z", "efficiency_score"):
        s = db[col].astype(float)
        dist[col] = {f"p{int(x*100)}": round(float(s.quantile(x)), 4) for x in q}

    def bucket_quality(col, bins, labels):
        out = []
        for lo, hi, lab in zip(bins[:-1], bins[1:], labels):
            m = (db[col] >= lo) & (db[col] < hi)
            if m.sum() == 0:
                continue
            out.append({
                "bucket": lab, "hubs": int(m.sum()),
                "median_best_win": round(float(db.loc[m, "best_win"].median()), 4),
                "median_lock_corr": round(float(db.loc[m, "mean_50_100_lock_corr"].median()), 4),
                "max_best_win": round(float(db.loc[m, "best_win"].max()), 4),
                "hubs_win_ge_60pct": int((db.loc[m, "best_win"] >= 0.60).sum()),
            })
        return out

    by_peers = bucket_quality("covariant_peer_count", [12, 25, 50, 100, 250, 1000, 1e9],
                              ["12-24", "25-49", "50-99", "100-249", "250-999", "1000+"])
    by_r = bucket_quality("mean_0_50_similarity", [0.85, 0.88, 0.90, 0.92, 0.94, 0.96, 1.01],
                          ["0.85-0.88", "0.88-0.90", "0.90-0.92", "0.92-0.94", "0.94-0.96", "0.96+"])

    report = {
        "generated_from": "phase2_covariant_db.parquet",
        "coverage": {"cells": summ["cells_total"], "hubs": len(db),
                     "hub_rate_pct": summ["hub_rate_pct"],
                     "edges": int(len(edges)), "vault_strategies": 0 if vault is None else int(len(vault))},
        "answers": {
            "highest_covariant_similarity": summ["covariant_similarity"],
            "strongest_continuation_lock": summ["continuation_lock"],
            "best_verified_edge": {
                "max_protected_win": round(float(db["protected_ext_win_rate"].max()), 4),
                "max_directional_win": round(float(db["directional_50_100_win_rate"].max()), 4),
                "best_combined_win": round(float(db["best_win"].max()), 4),
                "anchor_cell_id": int(db.loc[db["best_win"].idxmax(), "anchor_cell_id"]),
            },
            "statistically_significant_hubs_z5": int((db["best_z"] >= 5).sum()),
            "hubs_win_ge_60pct": int((db["best_win"] >= 0.60).sum()),
            "hubs_win_ge_70pct": int((db["best_win"] >= 0.70).sum()),
        },
        "distributions": dist,
        "efficiency_by_peer_count": by_peers,
        "efficiency_by_similarity": by_r,
        "top_25_by_efficiency": db.sort_values("best_z", ascending=False).head(25)[
            ["anchor_cell_id", "covariant_peer_count", "mean_0_50_similarity", "max_0_50_similarity",
             "mean_50_100_lock_corr", "frac_75_100_exact_lock", "protected_ext_win_rate",
             "directional_50_100_win_rate", "best_win", "best_z", "recommended_action"]
        ].round(4).to_dict(orient="records"),
        "edge_table": {
            "median_cov": round(float(edges["cov_0_50"].abs().median()), 4) if len(edges) else None,
            "p90_cov": round(float(edges["cov_0_50"].abs().quantile(0.9)), 4) if len(edges) else None,
            "max_cov": round(float(edges["cov_0_50"].abs().max()), 4) if len(edges) else None,
            "median_abs_lock": round(float(edges["lock_50_100"].abs().median()), 4) if len(edges) else None,
            "edges_lock_ge_065": int((edges["lock_50_100"].abs() >= 0.65).sum()) if len(edges) else 0,
        },
        "verdict": None,
