    }
    best = report["answers"]["best_verified_edge"]["best_combined_win"]
    report["verdict"] = (
        "Phase 2 produces tradeable structure: "
        f"{report['answers']['hubs_win_ge_60pct']:,} of {len(db):,} hubs exceed 60% out-of-sample win rates "
        f"(best {best:.1%}, {report['answers']['statistically_significant_hubs_z5']:,} hubs at z>=5)."
        if best >= 0.60 else
        f"No hub clears a robust 60% out-of-sample edge (best {best:.1%}); Phase 2 shows covariant structure but no proven directional edge."
    )

    for name in ("czet_eval.json", "czet_train_log.json", "null_test.json",
                 "fractal_report.json", "phase2_enrichment_report.json",
                 "crawled_math_operators_manifest.json"):
        pth = os.path.join(BASE, name)
        if os.path.exists(pth):
            with open(pth) as f:
                report[name.replace(".json", "")] = json.load(f)

    cop = os.path.join(BASE, "crawled_math_operators.npz")
    if os.path.exists(cop) and vault is not None and len(vault):
        zc = np.load(cop)
        ai = vault["anchor_cell_id"].to_numpy(np.int64) - 1
        rows = []
        for n in zc.files:
            v = zc[n]
            vh = v[ai]
            rows.append({
                "operator": n,
                "all_median": round(float(np.nanmedian(v)), 5),
                "all_p05": round(float(np.nanpercentile(v, 5)), 5),
                "all_p95": round(float(np.nanpercentile(v, 95)), 5),
                "vault_median": round(float(np.nanmedian(vh)), 5),
                "corr_vs_outbound_edge": round(float(np.corrcoef(vh, vault["outbound_verified_edge"])[0, 1]), 4),
            })
        report["crawled_operators"] = rows

    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_anomaly_efficiency_report.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in ("coverage", "answers", "edge_table", "verdict")}, indent=2))

    try:
        with pd.ExcelWriter(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_anomaly_efficiency_report.xlsx"),
                            engine="openpyxl") as w:
            pd.DataFrame([report["coverage"]]).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Coverage", index=False)
            pd.DataFrame(report["answers"]["best_verified_edge"], index=["value"]).T.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Answers")
            pd.DataFrame(dist).T.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Distributions")
            pd.DataFrame(by_peers).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Efficiency_by_Peers", index=False)
            pd.DataFrame(by_r).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Efficiency_by_Sim", index=False)
            db.sort_values("best_z", ascending=False).head(10000).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Top_Hubs", index=False)
            if vault is not None and len(vault):
                vault.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Strategy_Vault", index=False)
            if "crawled_operators" in report:
                pd.DataFrame(report["crawled_operators"]).to_excel(w, sheet_name="Crawled_Operators", index=False)
            cz = report.get("czet_eval", {})
            if cz:
                pd.DataFrame([cz]).T.to_excel(w, sheet_name="CZET_Eval")
            nt = report.get("null_test", {})
            if nt:
                pd.DataFrame([{k: (str(v) if isinstance(v, (dict, list)) else v) for k, v in nt.items()}]).T.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Null_Test")
            fr = report.get("fractal_report")
            if isinstance(fr, dict):
                flat = {k: (str(v) if isinstance(v, (dict, list)) else v) for k, v in fr.items()}
                pd.DataFrame([flat]).T.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Fractal_vs_Macro")
        print("[+] xlsx written")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[-] xlsx skipped: {type(e).__name__} {e}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
phase2/phase2_gatekeeper_verifier.py - the ten Phase 2 checks (129 lines, 6.2 KB)
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat

_orig_read_parquet = pd.read_parquet


def _read_parquet(p, *a, **k):
    try:
        return _orig_read_parquet(p, *a, **k)
    except Exception:
        return pqcompat.read_parquet(p)


pd.read_parquet = _read_parquet

PHASE2_DIR = "C:/cluster/phase2"

def run_phase2_gatekeeper_audit():
    print("=" * 78)
    print("         PHASE 2 MASTER GATEKEEPER VERIFICATION CHECKLIST")
    print("=" * 78)
    results = {}

    def check(item_id, name, passed, detail):
        status = "PASS" if passed else "MISSING / INCOMPLETE"
        icon = "[+]" if passed else "[!]"
        print(f"{icon} {item_id}. {name:<42} : {status} ({detail})")
        results[item_id] = {"name": name, "passed": bool(passed), "detail": detail}

    # 1. Subzones File & Contiguity
    npz_path = os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "jump10_phase2_subzones.npz")
    if os.path.exists(npz_path):
        z = np.load(npz_path, mmap_mode="r")
        keys = list(z.keys())
        n_rows = z["desc_Q1"].shape[0] if "desc_Q1" in keys else 0
        check("01", "591,171 Contiguous Cell Coverage", n_rows == 591171, f"Rows={n_rows:,}, Keys={len(keys)}")

        # 2. Structural Quartiles (Q1..Q4)
        has_q = all(k in keys for k in ["desc_Q1", "desc_Q2", "desc_Q3", "desc_Q4"])
        check("02", "0-25-50-75-100% & Fib Zone Decomposition", has_q, f"Q1..Q4 present={has_q}")

        # 3. 12-Angle Brute-Force Feature Density
        t_key = "trigger_vec_dense" if "trigger_vec_dense" in keys else ("trigger_vec" if "trigger_vec" in keys else None)
        t_dim = z[t_key].shape[1] if t_key else 0
        manifest_exists = os.path.exists(os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "crawled_math_operators_manifest.json"))
        check("03", "12-Angle Brute-Force + Webcrawl Features", t_dim >= 50 and manifest_exists,
              f"vec={t_key} (dim={t_dim}), webcrawl_manifest={manifest_exists}")

        # 4. Whitening & Bicoherence Fix Check
        bico = np.array(z["bicoherence"]) if "bicoherence" in keys else np.ones(10)
        bico_std = float(np.std(bico))
        T_sample = np.array(z[t_key][:2000])
        rand_sims = T_sample[:1000] @ T_sample[1000:2000].T
        mean_abs_r = float(np.mean(np.abs(rand_sims)))
        check("04", "Zero Collinear Collapse & Fixed Bicoherence", mean_abs_r < 0.25 and bico_std > 0.01,
              f"random |r|={mean_abs_r:.4f}, bico_std={bico_std:.4f}")
    else:
        for i in ["01", "02", "03", "04"]:
            check(i, "Subzone Tensor Checks", False, "jump10_phase2_subzones.npz missing")

    # 5. Relational Covariant DB & Positive/Negative Mirror Pairs
    cov_db_path = os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "phase2_covariant_db.parquet")
    edges_path = os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "phase2_covariant_edges.parquet")
    if os.path.exists(cov_db_path) and os.path.exists(edges_path):
        df_cov = pd.read_parquet(cov_db_path)
        df_edg = pd.read_parquet(edges_path)
        has_mirrors = ("pos_corr_peers" in df_cov.columns) and ("neg_mirror_peers" in df_cov.columns)
        check("05", "Relational Covariant DB (+/-0.85 Mirrors)", len(df_cov) > 1000 and has_mirrors,
              f"Hubs={len(df_cov):,}, Edges={len(df_edg):,}, Pos/Neg Mirrors={has_mirrors}")
    else:
        check("05", "Relational Covariant DB (+/-0.85 Mirrors)", False, "Parquet files missing")

    # 6. Inbound Pass 1 & Pass 2 Neural Convergence (CZET + Learnable Kernels)
    czet_eval_path = os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "czet_eval.json")
    if os.path.exists(czet_eval_path):
        with open(czet_eval_path, "r") as f:
            ev = json.load(f)
        spearman = float(ev.get("spearman", ev.get("pair_spearman", 0.15)))
        r2_val = float(ev.get("r2", ev.get("oos_r2", 0.0)))
        check("06", "CZET Inbound Pass 1&2 Continuation Ranking", spearman >= 0.28 and r2_val > 0.02,
              f"Spearman={spearman:.3f}, R2={r2_val:.4f} (Needs LearnableKernel/SweetSpot retraining if low)")
    else:
        check("06", "CZET Inbound Pass 1&2 Continuation Ranking", False, "czet_eval.json missing")

    # 7, 8, 9, 10. Strategy Vault, Fractal Multi-Timeframe, Indicator Coupling & Null Test
    vault_path = os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet")
    if os.path.exists(vault_path):
        df_v = pd.read_parquet(vault_path)
        cols = set(df_v.columns)
        check("07", "Outbound Streaming Strategy Vault (>=55%)", len(df_v) >= 100, f"Vault Strategies={len(df_v):,}")

        has_fractal = any("fractal" in c.lower() or "macro" in c.lower() for c in cols)
        check("08", "Multi-Timeframe Fractal Check (150t vs 1800t)", has_fractal,
              f"Fractal column present={has_fractal}")

        has_ind = any(k in c.lower() for c in cols for k in ["rsi", "vwap", "indicator", "divergence"])
        check("09", "Coupled Indicator Functions (RSI/VWAP)", has_ind,
              f"Indicator confluence columns present={has_ind}")

        has_null = any("null" in c.lower() or "pval" in c.lower() or "wilson" in c.lower() for c in cols)
        check("10", "Statistical Null / Wilson Lower Bound Audit", has_null,
              f"Statistical bound present={has_null}")
    else:
        for i, lbl in [("07", "Outbound Strategy Vault"), ("08", "Multi-TF Fractal Check"),
                       ("09", "Coupled Indicator Functions"), ("10", "Statistical Null Audit")]:
            check(i, lbl, False, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet missing")

    all_passed = all(v["passed"] for v in results.values())
    print("=" * 78)
    if all_passed:
        print("[VERDICT] PHASE 2 IS 100% COMPLETE & VERIFIED. CLEAR TO LAUNCH PHASE 3!")
    else:
        missing = [k for k, v in results.items() if not v["passed"]]
        print(f"[VERDICT] PHASE 2 HAS {len(missing)} UNFINISHED ITEM(S): {missing}")
        print("          Complete the missing items above before moving to Phase 3.")
    print("=" * 78)

    with open(os.path.join(PHASE2_DIR, "phase2_gatekeeper_status.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump({"all_passed": all_passed, "checks": results}, f, indent=2)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_phase2_gatekeeper_audit()
phase2/phase2_vault_enrich.py - indicator confluence columns (276 lines, 12.3 KB)
"""Phase 2 vault enrichment: items 08 (macro fractal), 09 (RSI/VWAP), 10 (null test).

Inputs : jump10_phase2_scan.npz, jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz (raw_tick_cells),
         phase2_covariant_db.parquet, phase2_strategy_vault.parquet
Outputs: vault_triggers.npz, indicator_features.npz, phase2_strategy_vault.parquet (enriched),
         phase2_anomaly_strategies.xlsx, phase2_enrichment_report.json
"""
import os
import sys
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
import pqcompat
from phase2_dense_anomaly_bank import compute_quartile_brute_force_features

BASE = r"C:\cluster\phase2"
P1 = r"C:\cluster\phase1\final\jump10_phase1_fourier_functions.npz"
N = 591171
N_IN = 502495
BLOCK = 12
CHUNK = 40000


def read_table(p):
    return pqcompat.read_parquet(p) if p.endswith(".parquet") else pd.read_csv(p)


def recompute_triggers(thr=0.85, min_hits=10, top_hubs=5000, batch=256):
    scan = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "jump10_phase2_scan.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    T = scan["trigger_vec_dense"]
    db = read_table(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_covariant_db.parquet"))
    db = db[db.anchor_cell_id <= N_IN].sort_values("anomaly_z_score", ascending=False).reset_index(drop=True)
    hubs = db.head(top_hubs)
    vault = read_table(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))
    pos = {int(a): i for i, a in enumerate(hubs.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy())}
    want = [pos[int(a)] for a in vault.anchor_cell_id]
    if any(int(a) not in pos for a in vault.anchor_cell_id):
        raise RuntimeError("vault anchor not in top_hubs set")
    Th = np.ascontiguousarray(T[[int(a) - 1 for a in hubs.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy()]])

    out_idx, out_pol, out_off = [], [], [0]
    for b0 in range(0, len(want), batch):
        sel = want[b0:b0 + batch]
        hits = [[] for _ in sel]
        for s in range(N_IN, N, 20000):
            e = min(N, s + 20000)
            sims = T[s:e] @ Th[sel].T
            big = np.abs(sims) >= thr
            if big.any():
                ri, ci = np.nonzero(big)
                for r, c in zip(ri, ci):
                    hits[c].append((s + r, sims[r, c]))
        for lst in hits:
            idx = np.fromiter((x[0] for x in lst), dtype=np.int64, count=len(lst))
            pol = np.fromiter((np.sign(x[1]) for x in lst), dtype=np.float32, count=len(lst))
            out_idx.append(idx)
            out_pol.append(pol)
            out_off.append(out_off[-1] + len(idx))
        print(f"    triggers {b0 + len(sel)}/{len(want)}", flush=True)
    np.savez(os.path.join(BASE, "vault_triggers.npz"),
             idx=np.concatenate(out_idx) if out_idx else np.zeros(0, np.int64),
             pol=np.concatenate(out_pol) if out_pol else np.zeros(0, np.float32),
             off=np.asarray(out_off, np.int64),
             hubs=vault.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy(np.int64))
    return vault, out_idx, out_pol


def indicator_block():
    cache = os.path.join(BASE, "indicator_features.npz")
    if os.path.exists(cache):
        d = np.load(cache)
        return {k: d[k] for k in d.files}
    z1 = np.load(P1, mmap_mode="r")
    px = z1["raw_tick_cells"]
    out = {}
    names = ["rsi_end1", "rsi_end2", "rsi_max1", "rsi_max2", "rsi_min1", "rsi_min2",
             "px_high1", "px_high2", "px_low1", "px_low2", "vwap_z1", "vwap_z2", "vwap_stretch1"]
    for n in names:
        out[n] = np.lib.format.open_memmap(os.path.join(BASE, f"_ind_{n}.npy"), mode="w+",
                                           dtype=np.float32, shape=(N,))
    W = 14
    for s in range(0, N, CHUNK):
        e = min(N, s + CHUNK)
        p = np.asarray(px[s:e], dtype=np.float64)
        d = np.diff(p, axis=1)
        up = np.clip(d, 0.0, None)
        dn = np.clip(-d, 0.0, None)
        cu = np.cumsum(up, axis=1)
        cd = np.cumsum(dn, axis=1)
        cs_u = np.concatenate([np.zeros((e - s, 1)), cu], axis=1)
        cs_d = np.concatenate([np.zeros((e - s, 1)), cd], axis=1)
        su = cs_u[:, W:] - cs_u[:, :-W]
        sd = cs_d[:, W:] - cs_d[:, :-W]
        rsi = 100.0 * su / (su + sd + 1e-12)
        full = np.full((e - s, p.shape[1]), 50.0)
        full[:, W:] = rsi
        full[:, 1:W] = rsi[:, :1]
        h1, h2 = full[:, :75], full[:, 75:]
        out["rsi_end1"][s:e] = full[:, 74]
        out["rsi_end2"][s:e] = full[:, -1]
        out["rsi_max1"][s:e] = h1.max(1)
        out["rsi_max2"][s:e] = h2.max(1)
        out["rsi_min1"][s:e] = h1.min(1)
        out["rsi_min2"][s:e] = h2.min(1)
        out["px_high1"][s:e] = p[:, :75].max(1)
        out["px_high2"][s:e] = p[:, 75:].max(1)
        out["px_low1"][s:e] = p[:, :75].min(1)
        out["px_low2"][s:e] = p[:, 75:].min(1)
        csum = np.cumsum(p, axis=1)
        cs2 = np.cumsum(p * p, axis=1)
        cs_pad = np.concatenate([np.zeros((e - s, 1)), csum], axis=1)
        cs2_pad = np.concatenate([np.zeros((e - s, 1)), cs2], axis=1)
        VW = 55
        m = (cs_pad[:, VW:] - cs_pad[:, :-VW]) / VW
        v = (cs2_pad[:, VW:] - cs2_pad[:, :-VW]) / VW - m * m
        sdv = np.sqrt(np.maximum(v, 1e-12))
        dev = p[:, VW - 1:] - m
        zsc = dev / sdv
        ncols = zsc.shape[1]
        fullz = np.zeros((e - s, p.shape[1]))
        fullz[:, VW - 1:] = zsc
        fullz[:, :VW - 1] = zsc[:, :1]
        out["vwap_z1"][s:e] = fullz[:, 74]
        out["vwap_z2"][s:e] = fullz[:, -1]
        out["vwap_stretch1"][s:e] = np.abs(fullz[:, :75]).max(1)
        print(f"    indicators {e:,}/{N:,} ({ncols} cols)", flush=True)
    for n in names:
        out[n].flush()
    np.savez(os.path.join(BASE, "indicator_features.npz"), **{n: np.asarray(out[n]) for n in names})
    return {n: np.asarray(out[n]) for n in names}


def macro_fractal(vault_anchor):
    z1 = np.load(P1, mmap_mode="r")
    px = z1["raw_tick_cells"]
    n_blocks = int(np.ceil(N / BLOCK))
    cached = os.path.join(BASE, "_macro_desc.npy")
    if os.path.exists(cached):
        macro_desc = np.load(cached)
    else:
      macro_desc = np.lib.format.open_memmap(cached, mode="w+", dtype=np.float32, shape=(n_blocks, 64))
      for b0 in range(0, n_blocks, 4000):
        b1 = min(n_blocks, b0 + 4000)
        rows = np.empty((b1 - b0, 150), dtype=np.float64)
        for i, bi in enumerate(range(b0, b1)):
            s = bi * BLOCK
            e = min(N, s + BLOCK)
            blk = np.asarray(px[s:e], dtype=np.float64)
            if blk.shape[0] < BLOCK:
                blk = np.vstack([blk, np.repeat(blk[-1:], BLOCK - blk.shape[0], axis=0)])
            rows[i] = blk.reshape(BLOCK, 150).mean(axis=0)
        macro_desc[b0:b1] = compute_quartile_brute_force_features(rows)
        print(f"    macro desc {b1:,}/{n_blocks:,}", flush=True)
    macro_desc = np.asarray(macro_desc)

    hub_idx = vault_anchor.astype(np.int64) - 1
    micro = np.empty((len(hub_idx), 150), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(0, len(hub_idx), 2000):
        sl = hub_idx[i:i + 2000]
        micro[i:i + 2000] = np.asarray(px[sl], dtype=np.float64)
    micro_desc = compute_quartile_brute_force_features(micro)
    blocks = hub_idx // BLOCK

    def cos64(a, b):
        an = (a - a.mean(1, keepdims=True)) / (a.std(1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
        bn = (b - b.mean(1, keepdims=True)) / (b.std(1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9)
        return np.sum(an * bn, axis=1) / a.shape[1]

    agree = cos64(micro_desc, macro_desc[blocks])
    return agree, micro_desc, macro_desc


def main():
    vault, trig_idx, trig_pol = recompute_triggers()
    print(f"[*] vault rows={len(vault)} triggers={len(trig_idx)}", flush=True)
    scan = np.load(os.path.join(BASE, "jump10_phase2_scan.npz"), mmap_mode="r")
    held = np.asarray(scan["protected_held"])
    ret = np.asarray(scan["q4_net_return"])

    print("[*] item 08 macro fractal ...", flush=True)
    agree, _, _ = macro_fractal(vault.anchor_cell_id.to_numpy(np.int64))
    is_frac = agree >= float(np.quantile(agree, 0.90))
    print(f"    agreement med={np.median(agree):.4f} p90={np.quantile(agree,0.9):.4f} "
          f"frac_anomalies={int(is_frac.sum())}", flush=True)

    print("[*] item 09 indicator block ...", flush=True)
    ind = indicator_block()

    print("[*] item 10 block-permutation null ...", flush=True)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20261025)
    nb = N - N_IN
    n_perm = 200
    n_full = nb // 150
    idx_base = (np.arange(n_full)[:, None] * 150 + np.arange(150)[None, :]).ravel()
    tail = np.arange(n_full * 150, nb, dtype=np.int64)
    perms = np.empty((n_perm, len(trig_idx)), dtype=np.float32)
    perm_held = np.empty((n_perm, len(trig_idx)), dtype=np.float32)
    held_o = held[N_IN:]
    ret_o = ret[N_IN:]
    li_all = [idx - N_IN for idx in trig_idx]
    for p in range(n_perm):
        pm = rng.permutation(n_full)
        newpos = np.concatenate([(pm[:, None] * 150 + np.arange(150)[None, :]).ravel(), tail])
        ph = held_o[newpos]
        pr = ret_o[newpos]
        for i, li in enumerate(li_all):
            perms[p, i] = float(np.mean(np.sign(pr[li] * trig_pol[i]) == 1))
            perm_held[p, i] = float(np.mean(ph[li]))
        if (p + 1) % 50 == 0:
            print(f"    perm {p + 1}/{n_perm}", flush=True)

    obs_dir, obs_prot, pvals = [], [], []
    for i, idx in enumerate(trig_idx):
        li = idx - N_IN
        od = float(np.mean(np.sign(ret[li] * trig_pol[i]) == 1))
        op = float(np.mean(held[li]))
        obs_dir.append(od)
        obs_prot.append(op)
        null = np.maximum(perms[:, i], perm_held[:, i])
        pvals.append(float((np.sum(null >= max(od, op)) + 1) / (n_perm + 1)))

    vault = vault.copy()
    vault["fractal_macro_agreement"] = np.round(agree, 4)
    vault["is_fractal_anomaly"] = is_frac
    vault["rsi_divergence_confluence"] = np.round(
        np.where((ind["px_high2"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1] > ind["px_high1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1])
                 & (ind["rsi_max2"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1] < ind["rsi_max1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1]), 1.0,
                 np.where((ind["px_low2"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1] < ind["px_low1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1])
                          & (ind["rsi_min2"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1] > ind["rsi_min1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1]), -1.0, 0.0)), 2)
    vault["vwap_band_anomaly_score"] = np.round(ind["vwap_stretch1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1], 3)
    vault["rsi_end1"] = np.round(ind["rsi_end1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1], 2)
    vault["vwap_z1"] = np.round(ind["vwap_z1"][vault.anchor_cell_id - 1], 3)
    vault["null_pval"] = np.round(pvals, 4)
    vault["null_p99"] = np.round(np.quantile(np.maximum(perms, perm_held), 0.99, axis=0), 4)
    vault["beats_null"] = vault["null_pval"] < 0.01
    vault["outbound_protected_win_rate"] = np.round(obs_prot, 4)
    vault["outbound_directional_win_rate"] = np.round(obs_dir, 4)
    vault["outbound_verified_edge"] = np.round(np.maximum(obs_prot, obs_dir), 4)

    pqcompat.write_parquet(vault, os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_strategy_vault.parquet"))
    try:
        with pd.ExcelWriter(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_anomaly_strategies.xlsx"), engine="openpyxl") as w:
            vault.to_excel(w, sheet_name="Phase3_Ready_Strategies", index=False)
            vault[vault["beats_null"]].to_excel(w, sheet_name="Null_Verified", index=False)
    except Exception as ex:
        print(f"[-] xlsx: {type(ex).__name__} {ex}", flush=True)

    top300 = vault.nlargest(min(300, len(vault)), "outbound_win_lb95")
    rep = {
        "vault_rows": int(len(vault)),
        "fractal": {"median_agreement": round(float(np.median(agree)), 4),
                    "p90_agreement": round(float(np.quantile(agree, 0.9)), 4),
                    "is_fractal_anomalies": int(is_frac.sum())},
        "indicators": {
            "rsi_divergence_bearish": int((vault["rsi_divergence_confluence"] == 1).sum()),
            "rsi_divergence_bullish": int((vault["rsi_divergence_confluence"] == -1).sum()),
            "vwap_stretch_median": round(float(vault["vwap_band_anomaly_score"].median()), 3),
        },
        "null_test": {
            "permutations": n_perm, "block_size_ticks": 150,
            "top300_observed_mean_win": round(float(top300["outbound_verified_edge"].mean()), 4),
            "top300_null_p99_mean": round(float(top300["null_p99"].mean()), 4),
            "top300_beats_null": int(top300["beats_null"].sum()),
            "vault_beats_null": int(vault["beats_null"].sum()),
            "median_pval": round(float(np.median(pvals)), 4),
        },
    }
    with open(os.path.join(BASE, "phase2_enrichment_report.json"), "w") as f:
        json.dump(rep, f, indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(rep, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
