Appendix C - metric definitions

Every statistic in this report, defined exactly as the code computes it, so that a reader can recompute any of them from the parquet.

C.1 Win rates

win_rate = (1/N) ∑ 1[unit_win_i]

For a directional contract unit_win = sign(gross_dir_149) == trade_dir. For ONETOUCH unit_win = max_excursion ≥ 0.75. For NOTOUCH unit_win = min_excursion > −1.0. For BARRIER unit_win = gross_dir_149 ≥ 0.35.

C.2 Wilson lower bound

LB = (p̂ + z²/2n − z√(p̂(1−p̂)/n + z²/4n²)) / (1 + z²/n)

with z = 1.959964. Used everywhere a hit rate is gated, because it penalises small samples automatically: an anchor with 12 hits needs a much higher point estimate to clear the same gate as one with 400.

C.3 Expected value and the gate

EV = p_WLB · R − 1, gate: EV > 0.02

where R is the contract payout and p_WLB the Wilson bound of the inbound hit rate for that contract family.

C.4 Profit factor

PF = ∑ pnl_i [pnl_i > 0] / |∑ pnl_i [pnl_i < 0]|

Computed on the friction-tier PnL column, not on gross sigma.

C.5 Sharpe, Sortino, Calmar

Sharpe = mean(pnl) / std(pnl) · √N Sortino = mean(pnl) / std(min(pnl, 0)) · √N Calmar = total_return / |max_drawdown|

Per-trade series, annualised by trade count rather than by calendar time, because the corpus is not uniformly sampled in wall-clock terms.

C.6 Kelly and the stake clamp

f* = (p_WLB · R − 1) / (R − 1) stake = clip(0.25 · f* · balance, 0.50, 0.01 · balance)

f* is set to zero when p_WLB · R ≤ 1, which is why the portfolio reports a Kelly fraction of zero.

C.7 Winsorised sigma

σ = 1.4826 · median(|Δmid| capped at 6.984375 · median(|Δmid|))

over the trailing 1,800 ticks, expressed in bps of price. The 1.4826 factor converts a median absolute deviation to a normal-consistent standard deviation; the cap is set at six median absolute tick moves so that Jump-10's discrete 10-point jumps do not dominate.

C.8 Monte Carlo

5,000 block-bootstrap runs with block length 50 trades, sampling with replacement from the realised trade sequence, re-compounding equity from zero and reporting the 95th/99th percentile worst drawdown, the median profit factor and P(final > 0).

C.9 Whitening

z = Λ−1/2 UT (x − μ)

with μ the column means, U the eigenvectors of the covariance and Λ the eigenvalues, keeping the 132 components after dropping three constant columns and removing the first (70.3% variance) component.

C.10 Anchor orthogonality

Two anchors are considered the same anomaly when |cos(z_i, z_j)| ≥ 0.82 in the 132-d space. Suppression keeps the anchor with the higher inbound Wilson bound. The gatekeeper asserts the surviving maximum is below the threshold.

Appendix D - glossary

TermDefinition as used in this project
Cell150 consecutive ticks. The unit of everything: one cell, one possible trade.
Quartile (Q1-Q4)37-38 tick sub-sequences of a cell. Q1+Q2 are observable; Q3+Q4 are forward.
Observable halfTicks 0-74. The only data available at decision time.
Forward halfTicks 75-149. Where every outcome is measured.
HubAn inbound cell with an unusually large number of similar peers (top 5,000 by anomaly z-score).
Covariant edge|r| ≥ 0.85 between the observable halves of two cells.
Peer countNumber of cells matching an anchor above the similarity threshold.
TriggerAn outbound cell that matches an anchor and therefore generates a candidate trade.
Lock correlationCorrelation between the forward halves of an anchor and a peer: does the future continue the same way?
Protected extremumThe forward path never breaks the far barrier; the quantity ONETOUCH/NOTOUCH monetise.
SigmaCausal cell volatility in bps, from the winsorised MAD estimator over the trailing 1,800 ticks.
Contract auctionChoosing, per anchor, the contract family with the highest gated EV.
Friction tierA cost level (0.00/0.03/0.06/0.10 sigma) subtracted from the realised excursion.
DeduplicationKeeping only the highest-EV candidate per outbound cell.
Selection artefactAn apparent edge produced by taking a maximum over noisy estimates rather than by the data.
Wilson boundLower confidence bound on a binomial rate, used as the gate input instead of the point estimate.
Live parityRecall of the live matcher against the backtest's trigger set. Currently 0.240.
GatekeeperAn executable checklist over shipped artefacts; 10 checks in Phase 2, 18 in Phase 3.
FableThe name for the Phase 3 sigma-survival framework and its seven live guards.
